CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS `notifications` (
  `id` int(11) NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  `nic` varchar(20) NOT NULL,
  `title` varchar(200) DEFAULT NULL,
  `message` text DEFAULT NULL,
  `is_read` tinyint(1) DEFAULT 0,
  `created_at` timestamp NOT NULL DEFAULT current_timestamp(),
  PRIMARY KEY (`id`),
  KEY `nic` (`nic`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB AUTO_INCREMENT=17 DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_general_ci;

-- --------------------------------------------------------

--
-- Table structure for table `users`
--

DROP TABLE IF EXISTS `users`;
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS `users` (
  `nic` varchar(20) NOT NULL,
  `name` varchar(100) NOT NULL,
  `email` varchar(100) NOT NULL,
  `password` varchar(255) NOT NULL,
  `computer_number` varchar(50) DEFAULT NULL,
  `mobile` varchar(15) DEFAULT NULL,
  `is_active` tinyint(1) DEFAULT 1,
  `created_at` timestamp NOT NULL DEFAULT current_timestamp(),
  `updated_at` timestamp NOT NULL DEFAULT current_timestamp() ON UPDATE current_timestamp(),
  `applicant_initials` varchar(50) DEFAULT NULL,
  `applicant_current_work_station` varchar(100) DEFAULT NULL,
  `applicant_service_number` varchar(30) DEFAULT NULL,
  `applicant_salary_sheet_number` varchar(30) DEFAULT NULL,
  `applicant_date_of_birth` date DEFAULT NULL,
  `applicant_private_address` text DEFAULT NULL,
  `applicant_official_address` text DEFAULT NULL,
  `applicant_civil_status` enum('Married','Unmarried','Divorced','Widowed') DEFAULT NULL,
  `applicant_designation` varchar(100) DEFAULT NULL,
  `applicant_current_sub_department` varchar(100) DEFAULT NULL,
  `applicant_salary_account_branch` varchar(100) DEFAULT NULL,
  `applicant_personal_file_office` varchar(100) DEFAULT NULL,
  `is_applicant_service_confirmed` tinyint(1) DEFAULT 0,
  `applicant_service_confirmed_date` date DEFAULT NULL,
  `applicant_gov_first_appointment_date` date DEFAULT NULL,
  `applicant_railway_first_appointment_date` date DEFAULT NULL,
  `applicant_current_sub_dept_duty_date` date DEFAULT NULL,
  `applicant_current_post_appointment_date` date DEFAULT NULL,

<?php
/**
 * Central Configuration - Change ONLY this file when moving to another computer
 * ============================================================================
 * TO RUN ON ANOTHER COMPUTER, CHANGE ONLY THE `BASE_URL` VALUE BELOW!
 * ============================================================================
 */

// ==========================================
// 1. BASE URL - CHANGE THIS ONE VALUE ONLY!
// ==========================================
// Examples:
//   - If your project is at: C:\xampp\htdocs\myproject\public
//     Set: define('BASE_URL', '/myproject');
//   
//   - If your project is at: C:\xampp\htdocs\QMS\applicants_dashboard\public
//     Set: define('BASE_URL', '/QMS/applicants_dashboard');
//   
//   - If your project is at the root: C:\xampp\htdocs\public
//     Set: define('BASE_URL', '');
//   
//   - If using virtual host: http://myapp.test
//     Set: define('BASE_URL', '');
//
// IMPORTANT: never put a space in this value, or in the folder name it
// points to under htdocs. A space gets URL-encoded to %20 in the browser,
// which breaks the string matching in public/index.php and causes an
// infinite redirect loop (ERR_TOO_MANY_REDIRECTS).

define('BASE_URL', '/Quarters_Management_System-V2/Quarters_Management_System-V2');  // ← CHANGE THIS ONLY! (must match your htdocs folder, no spaces)
//http://localhost/Quarters_Management_System-V2/Quarters_Management_System-V2/



// ==========================================
// 2. DATABASE CONFIGURATION
// ==========================================
define('DB_HOST', 'localhost');
define('DB_USER', 'root');
define('DB_PASS', '');
define('DB_NAME', 'qms_database_updated');

// ==========================================
// 3. APPLICATION SETTINGS
// ==========================================
define('APP_NAME', 'Quarter Management System');
define('APP_VERSION', '1.0.0');

// ==========================================
// 4. SECURITY SETTINGS
// ==========================================
// Password hashing
define('PASSWORD_COST', 12);

// Session security
define('SESSION_LIFETIME', 1800); // 30 minutes
define('MAX_LOGIN_ATTEMPTS', 5);
define('LOGIN_LOCKOUT_TIME', 900); // 15 minutes

// CSRF protection
define('CSRF_TOKEN_LIFETIME', 3600); // 1 hour

// Password requirements
define('MIN_PASSWORD_LENGTH', 8);

// ==========================================
// 5. FUNCTIONS
// ==========================================

/**
 * Get the full base URL
 */
function baseUrl($path = '') {
    return BASE_URL . '/public/' . ltrim($path, '/');
}

/**
 * Get asset URL
 */
function assetUrl($path = '') {
    return BASE_URL . '/public/assets/' . ltrim($path, '/');
}

/**
 * Get database connection
 * @return mysqli
 */
function getDBConnection() {
    $conn = new mysqli(DB_HOST, DB_USER, DB_PASS, DB_NAME);
    
    if ($conn->connect_error) {
        die(json_encode([
            'success' => false,
            'error' => 'Database Connection Failed: ' . $conn->connect_error
        ]));
    }
    
    $conn->set_charset("utf8mb4");
    return $conn;
}

/**
 * Redirect to a URL
 */
function redirect($path = '') {
    header('Location: ' . baseUrl($path));
    exit();
}

/**
 * Get current URL
 */
function currentUrl() {
    return (isset($_SERVER['HTTPS']) ? 'https://' : 'http://') . 
           $_SERVER['HTTP_HOST'] . $_SERVER['REQUEST_URI'];
}
?>

/**
 * Main JavaScript file - handles all client-side functionality
 */

// ==========================================
// DROPDOWN FUNCTIONALITY
// ==========================================
function toggleDropdown() {
    const dropdown = document.getElementById("profileDropdown");
    if (dropdown) {
        dropdown.classList.toggle("hidden");
    }
}

// Close dropdown when clicking outside
document.addEventListener('click', function(event) {
    const container = event.target.closest('.profile-dropdown-container');
    if (!container) {
        const dropdown = document.getElementById("profileDropdown");
        if (dropdown && !dropdown.classList.contains('hidden')) {
            dropdown.classList.add('hidden');
        }
    }
});

// ==========================================
// FORM VALIDATION
// ==========================================
function validateForm(formId) {
    const form = document.getElementById(formId);
    if (!form) return true;
    
    const inputs = form.querySelectorAll('input[required], select[required]');
    let isValid = true;
    
    inputs.forEach(input => {
        if (!input.value.trim()) {
            input.classList.add('border-red-500');
            isValid = false;
        } else {
            input.classList.remove('border-red-500');
        }
    });
    
    return isValid;
}

// ==========================================
// TOAST NOTIFICATION
// ==========================================
function showToast(message, type = 'success') {
    const toast = document.getElementById('toast-message');
    if (!toast) return;
    
    const colors = {
        success: 'bg-green-600',
        error: 'bg-red-600',
        info: 'bg-blue-600'
    };
    
    toast.innerText = message;
    toast.className = `fixed top-5 left-1/2 transform -translate-x-1/2 -translate-y-10 ${colors[type] || colors.success} text-white px-6 py-3 rounded-lg shadow-lg border border-amber-400 opacity-0 transition-all duration-300 z-50`;
    
    setTimeout(() => {
        toast.classList.remove('opacity-0', '-translate-y-10');
        toast.classList.add('opacity-100', 'translate-y-0');
    }, 50);
    
    setTimeout(() => {
        toast.classList.remove('opacity-100', 'translate-y-0');
        toast.classList.add('opacity-0', '-translate-y-10');
    }, 3000);
}

// ==========================================
// AJAX HELPERS
// ==========================================
function ajaxRequest(url, data, method = 'POST') {
    return fetch(url, {
        method: method,
        headers: {
            'Content-Type': 'application/x-www-form-urlencoded',
        },
        body: new URLSearchParams(data)
    })
    .then(response => response.json())
    .catch(error => {
        console.error('AJAX Error:', error);
        return { status: 'error', message: 'Connection error' };
    });
}

// ==========================================
// NOTIFICATION HANDLERS (AJAX)
// ==========================================
function handleAction(id, action) {
    const data = {
        ajax_action: action,
        action_id: id
    };
    
    ajaxRequest('', data)
        .then(response => {
            if (response.status === 'success') {
                showToast(response.message);
                
                if (action === 'mark_read') {
                    const statusEl = document.getElementById('status-text-' + id);
                    if (statusEl) {
                        statusEl.innerHTML = '<span class="text-xs font-semibold text-green-600 bg-green-50 px-3 py-1 rounded-full border border-green-200"><i class="fas fa-check-circle mr-1"></i> Read</span>';
                    }
                    const btnWrap = document.getElementById('mark-read-btn-wrap-' + id);
                    if (btnWrap) btnWrap.innerHTML = '';
                } else if (action === 'delete') {
                    const box = document.getElementById('notif-box-' + id);
                    if (box) box.remove();
                    
                    const container = document.getElementById('notificationsContainer');
                    if (container && container.querySelectorAll('[id^="notif-box-"]').length === 0) {
                        location.reload();
                    }
                }
            } else {
                showToast('❌ ' + (response.message || 'Action failed!'), 'error');
            }
        });
}

// ==========================================
// LOADING STATE
// ==========================================
function showLoading(button) {
    if (button) {
        button.disabled = true;
        button.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-spinner fa-spin mr-2"></i> Loading...';
    }
}

function hideLoading(button, originalText) {
    if (button) {
        button.disabled = false;
        button.innerHTML = originalText;
    }
}

// ==========================================
// INITIALIZATION
// ==========================================
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
    console.log('Quarter Management System loaded successfully');
});

<?php
/**
 * Front Controller - Entry point for all routes
 */

// Start session ONLY if not already started
if (session_status() === PHP_SESSION_NONE) {
    session_start();
}

// Error reporting for development
error_reporting(E_ALL);
ini_set('display_errors', 1);

// ==========================================
// LOAD CONFIGURATION
// ==========================================
require_once __DIR__ . '/../config/config.php';

// ==========================================
// AUTOLOADING
// ==========================================
spl_autoload_register(function ($class) {
    $base_dir = __DIR__ . '/../src/';
    $file = $base_dir . str_replace('\\', '/', $class) . '.php';
    if (file_exists($file)) {
        require_once $file;
    }
});

// ==========================================
// AUTHENTICATION CHECK
// ==========================================
function checkAuth() {
    // Get the current path without base URL
    // FIX: rawurldecode() so %20 (space) and other encoded chars match BASE_URL correctly
    $currentPath = rawurldecode($_SERVER['REQUEST_URI']);

    // Remove base URL from path
    if (strpos($currentPath, BASE_URL) === 0) {
        $currentPath = substr($currentPath, strlen(BASE_URL));
    }

    // Remove /public from path if present
    if (strpos($currentPath, '/public') === 0) {
        $currentPath = substr($currentPath, 7);
    }

    // Remove query string
    if (strpos($currentPath, '?') !== false) {
        $currentPath = strtok($currentPath, '?');
    }

    // Clean up the path
    $currentPath = rtrim($currentPath, '/');
    if (empty($currentPath)) {
        $currentPath = '/';
    }

    // Define public pages (no login required)
    $publicPaths = ['/', '/login', '/register', '/logout'];
    foreach ($publicPaths as $publicPath) {
        if ($currentPath === $publicPath || strpos($currentPath, $publicPath . '?') === 0) {
            return true;
        }
    }

    // If accessing public/index.php directly, allow it
    if (strpos($currentPath, '/public/index.php') !== false) {
        return true;
    }

    // Check if user is logged in
    if (!isset($_SESSION['nic']) || !isset($_SESSION['session_valid'])) {
        // Store the intended URL for redirect after login (CLEAN VERSION)
        // FIX: rawurldecode() here too, for the same reason as above
        $cleanPath = rawurldecode($_SERVER['REQUEST_URI']);
        // Remove base URL
        if (strpos($cleanPath, BASE_URL) === 0) {
            $cleanPath = substr($cleanPath, strlen(BASE_URL));
        }
        // Remove /public if present
        if (strpos($cleanPath, '/public') === 0) {
            $cleanPath = substr($cleanPath, 7);
        }
        // Remove query string if present
        if (strpos($cleanPath, '?') !== false) {
            $cleanPath = strtok($cleanPath, '?');
        }
        // Ensure it starts with /
        if (!empty($cleanPath) && $cleanPath[0] !== '/') {
            $cleanPath = '/' . $cleanPath;
        }
        $_SESSION['redirect_after_login'] = $cleanPath;

        header('Location: ' . baseUrl('login'));
        exit();
    }

    return true;
}

// ==========================================
// ROUTE HANDLER
// ==========================================
function route($path, $method = 'GET') {
    // FIX: decode the incoming path too, so BASE_URL stripping works the same way here
    $path = rawurldecode($path);

    // Remove query string
    if (strpos($path, '?') !== false) {
        $path = strtok($path, '?');
    }

    // Remove base path if running in subdirectory
    if (strpos($path, BASE_URL) === 0) {
        $path = substr($path, strlen(BASE_URL));
    }

    // Remove /public from path if present
    if (strpos($path, '/public') === 0) {
        $path = substr($path, 7);
    }

    if (empty($path) || $path === '/' || $path === '') {
        $path = '/home';
    }

    // Define routes - map URL paths to view files
    $routes = [
        // Existing routes
        '/home' => '/home.php',
        '/dashboard'                 => '/dashboard/index.php',
        '/application/request'       => '/dashboard/request_quarters.php',
        '/application/status'        => '/dashboard/view_status.php',
        '/waiting-list'              => '/dashboard/waiting_list.php',
        '/offer/respond'             => '/dashboard/view_offers.php',
        '/notifications'             => '/notifications/notification.php',
        '/profile/edit'              => '/profile/edit_profile.php',
        '/login'                     => '/auth/login.php',
        '/register'                  => '/auth/register.php',
        '/logout'                    => '/auth/logout.php',

        // --- NEW INQUIRY / COMPLAINT ROUTES ---
        '/complaints/new'           => '/dashboard/new_complaint.php',

        // Routes for the application forms and approvals
        '/application/select-language' => '/application/language_selector.php',
        '/application/residence' => '/application/residence.php',
        '/application/form/si'         => '/application/form_si.php',
        '/application/form/en'         => '/application/form_en.php',
        '/approval/deputy/en'          => '/approval/deputy_supt_en.php',
        '/approval/deputy/si'          => '/approval/deputy_supt_si.php',
        '/approval/clerk/en'           => '/approval/clerk_en.php',
        '/approval/clerk/si'           => '/approval/clerk_si.php',
        '/approval/marks'              => '/approval/marking_scheme.php',

        // +++ NEW ADMIN ROUTES +++
        '/admin/dashboard'            => '/admin/dashboard.php',
        '/admin/users'                => '/admin/users.php',
        '/admin/assign'               => '/admin/assign.php',
        '/admin/approvals'            => '/admin/approvals.php',
        '/admin/waiting-lists'        => '/admin/waiting_lists.php',
        '/admin/quarters'             => '/admin/quarters.php',
    ];

    // Handle AJAX requests first
    if ($method === 'POST' && isset($_POST['ajax_action'])) {
        handleAjaxRequest($_POST['ajax_action'], $_POST);
        return;
    }

    // ==========================================
    // CONTROLLER ROUTE HANDLING (e.g., complaints/store)
    // ==========================================
    if ($path === '/complaints/store' && $method === 'POST') {
        require_once __DIR__ . '/../src/controllers/ComplaintController.php';
        $controller = new ComplaintController();
        $controller->store();
        return;
    }

    if ($path === '/offer/respond' && $method === 'POST') {
        require_once __DIR__ . '/../src/controllers/OfferController.php';
        $controller = new OfferController();
        $controller->respond();
        return;
    }

    // ==========================================
// PREFERENCE SAVING ROUTES
// ==========================================
if ($path === '/application/save-residence' && $method === 'POST') {
    require_once __DIR__ . '/../src/controllers/PreferenceController.php';
    $controller = new PreferenceController();
    $controller->saveResidenceType();
    return;
}

if ($path === '/application/save-language' && $method === 'POST') {
    require_once __DIR__ . '/../src/controllers/PreferenceController.php';
    $controller = new PreferenceController();
    $controller->saveLanguage();
    return;
}

    $approvalActions = [
        '/approval/clerk/submit' => 'submitClerk',
        '/approval/deputy/submit' => 'submitSuperintendent',
        '/approval/marks/submit' => 'submitMarks'
    ];
    if ($method === 'POST' && isset($approvalActions[$path])) {
        require_once __DIR__ . '/../src/controllers/ApprovalController.php';
        try {
            $controller = new ApprovalController();
            $controller->{$approvalActions[$path]}();
        } catch (Throwable $error) {
            $_SESSION['approval_error'] = $error->getMessage();
            redirect('/dashboard');
        }
        return;
    }

    // Check if path is in routes
    if (isset($routes[$path])) {
        $viewPath = __DIR__ . '/../src/views' . $routes[$path];
        if (file_exists($viewPath)) {
            require_once $viewPath;
            return;
        }
    }

    // Handle direct PHP file requests
    if (strpos($path, '.php') !== false) {
        $viewPath = __DIR__ . '/../src/views' . $path;
        if (file_exists($viewPath)) {
            require_once $viewPath;
            return;
        }
    }

    // Handle root path
    if ($path === '/') {
        redirect('/dashboard');
        exit();
    }

    // 404 - Not Found
    http_response_code(404);
    echo '<h1>404 - Page Not Found</h1>';
    echo '<p>The page you are looking for does not exist.</p>';
    echo '<p><a href="' . baseUrl('/dashboard') . '">Go to Dashboard</a></p>';
}

// ==========================================
// AJAX REQUEST HANDLER
// ==========================================
function handleAjaxRequest($action, $data) {
    header('Content-Type: application/json');

    switch ($action) {
        case 'mark_read':
            require_once __DIR__ . '/../src/controllers/NotificationController.php';
            $controller = new NotificationController();
            $result = $controller->markAsRead($data['action_id'] ?? 0);
            echo json_encode($result);
            break;

        case 'delete':
            require_once __DIR__ . '/../src/controllers/NotificationController.php';
            $controller = new NotificationController();
            $result = $controller->delete($data['action_id'] ?? 0);
            echo json_encode($result);
            break;

        default:
            echo json_encode(['status' => 'error', 'message' => 'Invalid action']);
    }
}

// ==========================================
// EXECUTE ROUTING
// ==========================================

$requestUri = $_SERVER['REQUEST_URI'];
$requestMethod = $_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'];

// Check authentication for all pages
checkAuth();

// ==========================================
// NEW: Handle POST requests for form submission
// ==========================================
if ($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] === 'POST' && strpos(rawurldecode($_SERVER['REQUEST_URI']), '/application/submit') !== false) {
    require_once __DIR__ . '/../src/controllers/ApplicationController.php';
    $appController = new ApplicationController();
    $appController->submitFullApplication();
    exit();
}

// Route the request
route($requestUri, $requestMethod);
?>

================
File: README.md
================
# 🏠 Department of Railways - Quarter Management System

A web-based application for managing government quarter applications for Sri Lanka Railway employees.

## 📌 Features

- **User Authentication** - Secure login system
- **Quarter Applications** - Submit and track applications
- **Waiting List Management** - View position in queue
- **Status Tracking** - Monitor approval progress
- **Notifications** - Receive real-time updates
- **Profile Management** - Update user information
- **Offer Response** - Accept/Deny quarter offers

## 🏗️ Project Structure

================
File: src/controllers/ApplicationController.php
================
<?php
require_once __DIR__ . '/../models/Application.php';
require_once __DIR__ . '/../models/WaitingList.php';
require_once __DIR__ . '/../models/Notification.php';
require_once __DIR__ . '/AuthController.php';

class ApplicationController {
    private $auth;
    private $applicationModel;
    private $waitingListModel;
    private $notificationModel;
    
    public function __construct() {
        $this->auth = new AuthController();
        $this->auth->requireLogin();
        $this->applicationModel = new Application();
        $this->waitingListModel = new WaitingList();
        $this->notificationModel = new Notification();
    }
    
    /**
     * Submit new application
     */
    public function submit($quarterType) {
        $nic = $_SESSION['nic'];
        
        $result = $this->applicationModel->create($nic, $quarterType);
        
        if ($result['success']) {
            // Add to waiting list
            $position = $this->getNextWaitingListPosition($quarterType);
            $this->waitingListModel->add($nic, $quarterType, $position);
            
            // Create notification
            $this->notificationModel->create(
                $nic,
                'Application Received',
                'Your quarter application has been received and is under review.'
            );
            
            return [
                'success' => true,
                'message' => 'Your application has been submitted successfully!',
                'computer_no' => $result['computer_no']
            ];
        }
        
        return [
            'success' => false,
            'message' => 'Error submitting application. Please try again.'
        ];
    }
    
    /**
     * Get application status
     */
    public function getStatus($computerNo = null) {
        $nic = $_SESSION['nic'];
        
        if ($computerNo) {
            $application = $this->applicationModel->findByComputerNoAndNic($computerNo, $nic);
        } else {
            $application = $this->applicationModel->findByNic($nic);
        }
        
        if ($application) {
            $position = $this->waitingListModel->getPosition($nic, $application['quarter_type']);
            $application['position'] = $position;
            return $application;
        }
        
        return null;
    }
    
    /**
     * Get next waiting list position
     */
    private function getNextWaitingListPosition($quarterType) {
        $conn = getDBConnection();
        $stmt = $conn->prepare("SELECT COUNT(*) AS count FROM waiting_list WHERE quarter_type = ?");
        $stmt->bind_param("s", $quarterType);
        $stmt->execute();
        $row = $stmt->get_result()->fetch_assoc();
        $stmt->close();
        $conn->close();
        return ($row['count'] ?? 0) + 1;
    }

    /**
     * Process the full application form submission
     */
    public function submitFullApplication() {
        $this->auth->requireLogin();
        $nic = $_SESSION['nic'];
        $formLanguage = $_POST['form_language'] ?? 'en';
        $formPath = $formLanguage === 'single_residence' ? '/application/form/si' : '/application/form/en';

        $fieldAliases = [
            'full_name' => 'name',
            'national_id' => 'nic',
            'house_type' => 'quarter_type',
            'initials' => 'applicant_initials',
            'current_post' => 'applicant_current_work_station',
            'service_no' => 'applicant_service_number',
            'computer_no' => 'computer_number',
            'salary_slip_no' => 'applicant_salary_sheet_number',
            'dob' => 'applicant_date_of_birth',
            'phone_no' => 'mobile',
            'private_address' => 'applicant_private_address',
            'residential_address' => 'applicant_official_address',
            'civil_status' => 'applicant_civil_status',
            'designation' => 'applicant_designation',
            'sub_department' => 'applicant_current_sub_department',
            'salary_account' => 'applicant_salary_account_branch',
            'official_letter_office' => 'applicant_personal_file_office',
            'is_permanent' => 'is_applicant_service_confirmed',
            'confirmed_date' => 'applicant_service_confirmed_date',
            'gov_date' => 'applicant_gov_first_appointment_date',
            'railway_date' => 'applicant_railway_first_appointment_date',
            'subdept_date' => 'applicant_current_sub_dept_duty_date',
            'current_post_date' => 'applicant_current_post_appointment_date',
            'other_subdept' => 'has_applicant_served_other_sub_dept',
            'service_breaks' => 'has_applicant_service_breaks',
            'has_children' => 'does_applicant_have_children_residing',
            'has_spouse' => 'does_applicant_have_spouse_residing',
            'spouse_gov_job' => 'is_applicant_spouse_gov_employee',
            'is_rented_house' => 'is_applicant_currently_renting',
            'has_disability' => 'does_applicant_have_disability',
            'rejected_quarters' => 'has_applicant_rejected_quarter_before',
            'applied_before' => 'has_applicant_applied_before',
            'location' => 'applied_quarter_location',
            'house_no' => 'applied_quarter_number',
            'spouse_name' => 'spouse_full_name',
            'spouse_moving' => 'spouse_will_reside_in_quarters',
            'spouse_position' => 'spouse_designation',
            'spouse_institution' => 'spouse_institution_head_and_address',
            'spouse_current_post' => 'spouse_work_station_and_address',
            'permanent_address_sec6' => 'permanent_residence_address',
            'owner_is_self' => 'is_applicant_owner_of_residence',
            'owner_relationship' => 'owner_relationship_if_not_self',
            'commute_from_home' => 'does_applicant_commute_from_residence',
            'permanent_district' => 'permanent_residence_district',
            'nearest_city_name' => 'nearest_major_city_name',
            'nearest_city_distance' => 'distance_to_nearest_city_km',
            'branch_to_work_distance' => 'distance_from_branch_post_to_work_km',
            'house_owner' => 'rented_house_owner_name',
            'house_location' => 'rented_house_location',
            'rented_address' => 'rented_house_address',
            'rent_date' => 'rented_house_occupancy_date',
            'monthly_rent' => 'rented_house_monthly_rent',
            'disability_details' => 'disability_or_illness_details',
            'quarter_number' => 'previous_quarter_number',
            'moved_out_date' => 'previous_quarter_vacated_date',
            'reason_for_moving_out' => 'previous_quarter_vacate_reason',
            'applied_date_prev' => 'previous_application_date',
            'prev_house_type' => 'previous_applied_quarter_type',
            'prev_house_location' => 'previous_applied_quarter_location',
            'declaration_month' => 'declaration_salary_month',
            'declaration_year' => 'declaration_salary_year',
            'page3_date' => 'declaration_date',
            'child_full_name' => 'child_name',
            'child_dob' => 'child_date_of_birth'
        ];
        foreach ($fieldAliases as $field => $alias) {
            if (!isset($_POST[$field]) && isset($_POST[$alias])) {
                $_POST[$field] = $_POST[$alias];
            }
        }
        array_walk_recursive($_POST, static function (&$value) {
            if ($value === '') {
                $value = null;
            }
        });
        if (empty($_FILES['medical_cert']) && !empty($_FILES['medical_certificate_file_path'])) {
            $_FILES['medical_cert'] = $_FILES['medical_certificate_file_path'];
        }
        if (empty($_FILES['salary_cert']) && !empty($_FILES['salary_certificate_file_path'])) {
            $_FILES['salary_cert'] = $_FILES['salary_certificate_file_path'];
        }
        if (in_array($_POST['civil_status'] ?? '', ['විවාහක', 'අවිවාහක', 'දික්කසාදිත', 'වැන්දඹු'], true)) {
            $_POST['civil_status'] = [
                'විවාහක' => 'Married',
                'අවිවාහක' => 'Unmarried',
                'දික්කසාදිත' => 'Divorced',
                'වැන්දඹු' => 'Widowed'
            ][$_POST['civil_status']];
        }

        $required = ['full_name', 'national_id', 'designation', 'house_type'];
        foreach ($required as $field) {
            if (empty($_POST[$field])) {
                $_SESSION['form_error'] = "Please fill in all required fields.";
                redirect($formPath);
                return;
            }
        }

        // The NIC typed on the form must match the logged-in applicant's NIC.
        if (trim($_POST['national_id']) !== trim($nic)) {
            $_SESSION['form_error'] = "The NIC entered does not match your account. Please correct it.";
            redirect($formPath);
            return;
        }
        if (!in_array($formLanguage, ['en', 'single_residence'], true)) {
            $formLanguage = 'en';
            $formPath = '/application/form/en';
        }
        $residenceType = $_POST['residence_type'] ?? ($_SESSION['residence_type'] ?? 'single');
        if (!in_array($residenceType, ['single', 'joint_residence'], true)) {
            $residenceType = 'single';
        }
        $conn = getDBConnection();
        $conn->begin_transaction();

        try {
            // --- Assign all POST values to variables ---
            $full_name = $_POST['full_name'];
            $initials = $_POST['initials'] ?? null;
            $current_post = $_POST['current_post'] ?? null;
            $service_no = $_POST['service_no'] ?? null;
            $computer_no = $_POST['computer_no'] ?? $_SESSION['computer_number'] ?? null;
            $salary_slip_no = $_POST['salary_slip_no'] ?? null;
            $dob = $_POST['dob'] ?? null;
            $phone_no = $_POST['phone_no'] ?? null;
            $private_address = $_POST['private_address'] ?? null;
            $residential_address = $_POST['residential_address'] ?? null;
            $civil_status = $_POST['civil_status'] ?? null;
            $designation = $_POST['designation'] ?? null;
            $sub_department = $_POST['sub_department'] ?? null;
            $salary_account = $_POST['salary_account'] ?? null;
            $official_letter_office = $_POST['official_letter_office'] ?? null;
            $isConfirmed = ($_POST['is_permanent'] ?? 'no') === 'yes' ? 1 : 0;
            $confirmedDate = !empty($_POST['confirmed_date']) ? $_POST['confirmed_date'] : null;
            $gov_date = $_POST['gov_date'] ?? null;
            $railway_date = $_POST['railway_date'] ?? null;
            $subdept_date = $_POST['subdept_date'] ?? null;
            $current_post_date = $_POST['current_post_date'] ?? null;

            // 1. Update users table - 21 SET fields + 1 WHERE = 22 placeholders
            $updateUserSQL = "
                UPDATE users SET
                    name = ?,
                    applicant_initials = ?,
                    applicant_current_work_station = ?,
                    applicant_service_number = ?,
                    computer_number = ?,
                    applicant_salary_sheet_number = ?,
                    applicant_date_of_birth = ?,
                    mobile = ?,
                    applicant_private_address = ?,
                    applicant_official_address = ?,
                    applicant_civil_status = ?,
                    applicant_designation = ?,
                    applicant_current_sub_department = ?,
                    applicant_salary_account_branch = ?,
                    applicant_personal_file_office = ?,
                    is_applicant_service_confirmed = ?,
                    applicant_service_confirmed_date = ?,
                    applicant_gov_first_appointment_date = ?,
                    applicant_railway_first_appointment_date = ?,
                    applicant_current_sub_dept_duty_date = ?,
                    applicant_current_post_appointment_date = ?
                WHERE nic = ?
            ";
            $stmt = $conn->prepare($updateUserSQL);
            // 22 's' characters for 22 variables (21 SET fields + 1 WHERE)
            $stmt->bind_param(
                "ssssssssssssssssssssss", // This is 22 's' characters
                $full_name,
                $initials,
                $current_post,
                $service_no,
                $computer_no,
                $salary_slip_no,
                $dob,
                $phone_no,
                $private_address,
                $residential_address,
                $civil_status,
                $designation,
                $sub_department,
                $salary_account,
                $official_letter_office,
                $isConfirmed,
                $confirmedDate,
                $gov_date,
                $railway_date,
                $subdept_date,
                $current_post_date,
                $nic  // 22nd variable for WHERE clause
            );
            $stmt->execute();
            $stmt->close();

            // 2. Promote this user's saved draft, or create a submitted row if needed.
            $quarterType = $_POST['house_type'] ?? '';
            $quarterNumber = $_POST['house_no'] ?? null;
            $quarterLocation = $_POST['location'] ?? null;
            $computer_no_val = trim((string)($_POST['computer_no'] ?? $_SESSION['computer_number'] ?? ''));
            if ($computer_no_val === '') {
                $computerStmt = $conn->prepare("SELECT computer_number FROM users WHERE nic = ?");
                $computerStmt->bind_param("s", $nic);
                $computerStmt->execute();
                $computerRow = $computerStmt->get_result()->fetch_assoc();
                $computerStmt->close();
                $computer_no_val = $computerRow['computer_number'] ?? '';
            }
            if ($computer_no_val === '') {
                throw new RuntimeException('Your account does not have a computer number. Update your profile before applying.');
            }

            $hasOtherSub = isset($_POST['other_subdept']) && $_POST['other_subdept'] === 'yes' ? 1 : 0;
            $hasBreaks = isset($_POST['service_breaks']) && $_POST['service_breaks'] === 'yes' ? 1 : 0;
            $hasChildren = isset($_POST['has_children']) && $_POST['has_children'] === 'yes' ? 1 : 0;
            $hasSpouse = isset($_POST['has_spouse']) && $_POST['has_spouse'] === 'yes' ? 1 : 0;
            $spouseGov = isset($_POST['spouse_gov_job']) && $_POST['spouse_gov_job'] === 'yes' ? 1 : 0;
            $isRenting = isset($_POST['is_rented_house']) && $_POST['is_rented_house'] === 'yes' ? 1 : 0;
            $hasDisability = isset($_POST['has_disability']) && $_POST['has_disability'] === 'yes' ? 1 : 0;
            $rejectedBefore = isset($_POST['rejected_quarters']) && $_POST['rejected_quarters'] === 'yes' ? 1 : 0;
            $appliedBefore = isset($_POST['applied_before']) && $_POST['applied_before'] === 'yes' ? 1 : 0;

            $draftStmt = $conn->prepare(
                "SELECT id FROM applications WHERE nic = ? AND application_status = 'draft' ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1"
            );
            $draftStmt->bind_param("s", $nic);
            $draftStmt->execute();
            $draftRow = $draftStmt->get_result()->fetch_assoc();
            $draftStmt->close();

            if ($draftRow) {
                $applicationId = (int)$draftRow['id'];
                $appSQL = "
                    UPDATE applications SET
                        computer_no = ?, quarter_type = ?, application_date = CURDATE(),
                        applied_quarter_number = ?, applied_quarter_location = ?,
                        has_applicant_served_other_sub_dept = ?, has_applicant_service_breaks = ?,
                        does_applicant_have_children_residing = ?, does_applicant_have_spouse_residing = ?,
                        is_applicant_spouse_gov_employee = ?, is_applicant_currently_renting = ?,
                        does_applicant_have_disability = ?, has_applicant_rejected_quarter_before = ?,
                        has_applicant_applied_before = ?, application_status = 'submitted',
                        submit_status = 'success', status = 'pending', application_submitted_at = NOW()
                    WHERE id = ? AND nic = ? AND application_status = 'draft'
                ";
                $stmt = $conn->prepare($appSQL);
                $stmt->bind_param(
                    "ssssiiiiiiiiiis",
                    $computer_no_val,
                    $quarterType,
                    $quarterNumber,
                    $quarterLocation,
                    $hasOtherSub,
                    $hasBreaks,
                    $hasChildren,
                    $hasSpouse,
                    $spouseGov,
                    $isRenting,
                    $hasDisability,
                    $rejectedBefore,
                    $appliedBefore,
                    $applicationId,
                    $nic
                );
                $stmt->execute();
                $stmt->close();
            } else {
                $appSQL = "
                    INSERT INTO applications (
                        nic, computer_no, quarter_type, application_date,
                        applied_quarter_number, applied_quarter_location,
                        has_applicant_served_other_sub_dept, has_applicant_service_breaks,
                        does_applicant_have_children_residing, does_applicant_have_spouse_residing,
                        is_applicant_spouse_gov_employee, is_applicant_currently_renting,
                        does_applicant_have_disability, has_applicant_rejected_quarter_before,
                        has_applicant_applied_before, residence_type, form_language,
                        application_status, submit_status, status, application_submitted_at
                    ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, CURDATE(), ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 'submitted', 'success', 'pending', NOW())
                ";
                $stmt = $conn->prepare($appSQL);
                $stmt->bind_param(
                    "sssssiiiiiiiiiss",
                    $nic,
                    $computer_no_val,
                    $quarterType,
                    $quarterNumber,
                    $quarterLocation,
                    $hasOtherSub,
                    $hasBreaks,
                    $hasChildren,
                    $hasSpouse,
                    $spouseGov,
                    $isRenting,
                    $hasDisability,
                    $rejectedBefore,
                    $appliedBefore,
                    $residenceType,
                    $formLanguage
                );
                $stmt->execute();
                $applicationId = $stmt->insert_id;
                $stmt->close();
            }

            $receivedDate = $_POST['office_received_date'] ?? null;
            $receivedStmt = $conn->prepare("UPDATE applications SET application_date_received = ? WHERE id = ?");
            $receivedStmt->bind_param("si", $receivedDate, $applicationId);
            $receivedStmt->execute();
            $receivedStmt->close();

            // 3. Other sub‑departments served
            if ($hasOtherSub) {
                $names = $_POST['other_sub_dept_name'] ?? $_POST['table_v_dept'] ?? [];
                $froms = $_POST['other_sub_dept_from'] ?? $_POST['table_v_from'] ?? [];
                $tos = $_POST['other_sub_dept_to'] ?? $_POST['table_v_to'] ?? [];
                $sql = "INSERT INTO other_sub_departments_served (application_id, sub_department_name, service_from_date, service_to_date) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)";
                $stmt = $conn->prepare($sql);
                for ($i = 0; $i < count($names); $i++) {
                    $departmentName = trim((string)($names[$i] ?? ''));
                    $serviceFrom = $froms[$i] ?? '';
                    $serviceTo = $tos[$i] ?? '';
                    if ($departmentName !== '' && $serviceFrom !== '' && $serviceTo !== '') {
                        $stmt->bind_param("isss", $applicationId, $departmentName, $serviceFrom, $serviceTo);
                        $stmt->execute();
                    }
                }
                $stmt->close();
            }

            // 4. Service breaks
            if ($hasBreaks) {
                $names = $_POST['break_sub_dept'] ?? $_POST['table_vi_dept'] ?? [];
                $froms = $_POST['break_from'] ?? $_POST['table_vi_from'] ?? [];
                $tos = $_POST['break_to'] ?? $_POST['table_vi_to'] ?? [];
                $sql = "INSERT INTO service_breaks (application_id, break_sub_department, break_from_date, break_to_date) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)";
                $stmt = $conn->prepare($sql);
                for ($i = 0; $i < count($names); $i++) {
                    $departmentName = trim((string)($names[$i] ?? ''));
                    $breakFrom = $froms[$i] ?? '';
                    $breakTo = $tos[$i] ?? '';
                    if ($departmentName !== '' && $breakFrom !== '' && $breakTo !== '') {
                        $stmt->bind_param("isss", $applicationId, $departmentName, $breakFrom, $breakTo);
                        $stmt->execute();
                    }
                }
                $stmt->close();
            }

            // 5. Children details
            if ($hasChildren) {
                $names = $_POST['child_full_name'] ?? $_POST['child_name'] ?? [];
                $genders = $_POST['child_gender'] ?? [];
                $dobs = $_POST['child_dob'] ?? [];
                $statuses = $_POST['child_civil_status'] ?? [];
                $sql = "INSERT INTO children_details (application_id, child_full_name, child_gender, child_date_of_birth, child_civil_status) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)";
                $stmt = $conn->prepare($sql);
                for ($i = 0; $i < count($names); $i++) {
                    $childName = trim((string)($names[$i] ?? ''));
                    $childGender = $genders[$i] ?? '';
                    $childDob = $dobs[$i] ?? '';
                    $childStatus = $statuses[$i] ?? '';
                    if ($childName !== '' && in_array($childGender, ['female', 'male'], true) && $childDob !== '' && in_array($childStatus, ['married', 'unmarried', 'divorced', 'widowed'], true)) {
                        $stmt->bind_param("issss", $applicationId, $childName, $childGender, $childDob, $childStatus);
                        $stmt->execute();
                    }
                }
                $stmt->close();
            }

            // 6. Spouse details
            if ($hasSpouse) {
                $spouseSql = "INSERT INTO spouse_details (
                    application_id, spouse_full_name, spouse_national_id, spouse_occupation,
                    spouse_will_reside_in_quarters, spouse_department, spouse_service_number,
                    spouse_designation, spouse_institution_head_and_address,
                    spouse_work_station_and_address, spouse_annual_salary
                ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)";
                $stmt = $conn->prepare($spouseSql);
                $spouseName = trim((string)($_POST['spouse_name'] ?? ''));
                $spouseNic = trim((string)($_POST['spouse_national_id'] ?? ''));
                if ($spouseName === '' || $spouseNic === '') {
                    throw new RuntimeException('Enter the spouse name and NIC, or select that no spouse will reside in the quarter.');
                }
                $spouseOcc = $_POST['spouse_occupation'] ?? null;
                $spouseResideInput = $_POST['spouse_moving'] ?? '';
                $spouseReside = in_array($spouseResideInput, ['Will come', 'පැමිණේ'], true) ? 'Will Come' : (in_array($spouseResideInput, ['Will not come', 'පැමිණෙන්නේ නැත'], true) ? 'Will Not Come' : null);
                $spouseDept = $_POST['spouse_department'] ?? null;
                $spouseServNo = $_POST['spouse_service_number'] ?? null;
                $spouseDesig = $_POST['spouse_position'] ?? null;
                $spouseInst = $_POST['spouse_institution'] ?? null;
                $spouseWork = $_POST['spouse_current_post'] ?? null;
                $spouseSal = $_POST['spouse_annual_salary'] ?? null;
                $stmt->bind_param(
                    "issssssssss",
                    $applicationId, $spouseName, $spouseNic, $spouseOcc, $spouseReside,
                    $spouseDept, $spouseServNo, $spouseDesig, $spouseInst, $spouseWork, $spouseSal
                );
                $stmt->execute();
                $stmt->close();
            }

            // 7. Permanent residence details
            $permSql = "INSERT INTO permanent_residence_details (
                application_id, permanent_residence_address, is_applicant_owner_of_residence,
                owner_relationship_if_not_self, does_applicant_commute_from_residence,
                permanent_residence_district, nearest_major_city_name,
                distance_to_nearest_city_km, distance_from_branch_post_to_work_km
            ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)";
            $stmt = $conn->prepare($permSql);
            $permAddress = trim((string)($_POST['permanent_address_sec6'] ?? $_POST['permanent_address'] ?? ''));
            if ($permAddress === '') {
                throw new RuntimeException('Enter your permanent residence address.');
            }
            $isOwner = isset($_POST['owner_is_self']) && $_POST['owner_is_self'] === 'yes' ? 1 : 0;
            $ownerRel = $_POST['owner_relationship'] ?? null;
            $commute = isset($_POST['commute_from_home']) && $_POST['commute_from_home'] === 'yes' ? 1 : 0;
            $district = $_POST['permanent_district'] ?? null;
            $nearestCity = trim((string)($_POST['nearest_city_name'] ?? '')) ?: null;
            $distanceCity = ($_POST['nearest_city_distance'] ?? '') !== '' ? (float)$_POST['nearest_city_distance'] : null;
            $distanceBranch = ($_POST['branch_to_work_distance'] ?? '') !== '' ? (float)$_POST['branch_to_work_distance'] : null;
            $stmt->bind_param(
                "isisissdd",
                $applicationId, $permAddress, $isOwner, $ownerRel, $commute,
                $district, $nearestCity, $distanceCity, $distanceBranch
            );
            $stmt->execute();
            $stmt->close();

            $positionStmt = $conn->prepare("SELECT COUNT(*) + 1 AS next_position FROM waiting_list WHERE quarter_type = ?");
            $positionStmt->bind_param("s", $quarterType);
            $positionStmt->execute();
            $waitingListNo = (int)$positionStmt->get_result()->fetch_assoc()['next_position'];
            $positionStmt->close();

            $waitingStmt = $conn->prepare("INSERT INTO waiting_list (nic, position, quarter_type, applied_date) VALUES (?, ?, ?, CURDATE())");
            $waitingStmt->bind_param("sis", $nic, $waitingListNo, $quarterType);
            $waitingStmt->execute();
            $waitingStmt->close();

            $applicationListStmt = $conn->prepare("UPDATE applications SET waiting_list_no = ? WHERE id = ?");
            $applicationListStmt->bind_param("ii", $waitingListNo, $applicationId);
            $applicationListStmt->execute();
            $applicationListStmt->close();

            // 8. Rented house details
            if ($isRenting) {
                $rentSql = "INSERT INTO rented_house_details (
                    application_id, rented_house_owner_name, rented_house_location,
                    rented_house_address, rented_house_occupancy_date, rented_house_monthly_rent
                ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)";
                $stmt = $conn->prepare($rentSql);
                $owner = $_POST['house_owner'] ?? null;
                $location = $_POST['house_location'] ?? null;
                $address = $_POST['rented_address'] ?? null;
                $occDate = $_POST['rent_date'] ?? null;
                $rent = $_POST['monthly_rent'] ?? null;
                $stmt->bind_param("issssd", $applicationId, $owner, $location, $address, $occDate, $rent);
                $stmt->execute();
                $stmt->close();
            }

            // 9. Disability / illness details
            if ($hasDisability) {
                $medicalPath = $this->uploadFile('medical_cert', 'medical_certificates');
                $disSql = "INSERT INTO disability_medical_details (application_id, disability_or_illness_details, medical_certificate_file_path) VALUES (?, ?, ?)";
                $stmt = $conn->prepare($disSql);
                $details = $_POST['disability_details'] ?? null;
                $stmt->bind_param("iss", $applicationId, $details, $medicalPath);
                $stmt->execute();
                $stmt->close();
            }

            // 10. Previous quarter details
            $prevSql = "INSERT INTO previous_quarter_details (
                application_id, previous_quarter_number, previous_quarter_vacated_date,
                previous_quarter_vacate_reason, has_applicant_rejected_quarter_before,
                rejected_quarter_details, has_applicant_applied_for_quarter_before,
                previous_application_date, previous_applied_quarter_type,
                previous_applied_quarter_location
            ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)";
            $stmt = $conn->prepare($prevSql);
            $prevQuarterNo = $_POST['quarter_number'] ?? null;
            $vacatedDate = $_POST['moved_out_date'] ?? null;
            $vacateReason = $_POST['reason_for_moving_out'] ?? null;
            $rejectedDetails = $_POST['rejected_details'] ?? null;
            $prevAppDate = $_POST['applied_date_prev'] ?? null;
            $prevQuarterType = $_POST['prev_house_type'] ?? null;
            $prevLocation = $_POST['prev_house_location'] ?? null;
            $stmt->bind_param(
                "isssisssss",
                $applicationId, $prevQuarterNo, $vacatedDate, $vacateReason,
                $rejectedBefore, $rejectedDetails, $appliedBefore,
                $prevAppDate, $prevQuarterType, $prevLocation
            );
            $stmt->execute();
            $stmt->close();

            // 11. Declaration details
            $salaryPath = $this->uploadFile('salary_cert', 'salary_certificates');
            $decSql = "INSERT INTO declaration_details (
                application_id, declaration_salary_month, declaration_salary_year,
                salary_certificate_file_path, declaration_date
            ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)";
            $stmt = $conn->prepare($decSql);
            $month = $_POST['declaration_month'] ?? date('F');
            $year = (int)($_POST['declaration_year'] ?? date('Y'));
            $declDate = $_POST['page3_date'] ?? date('Y-m-d');
            $stmt->bind_param("isiss", $applicationId, $month, $year, $salaryPath, $declDate);
            $stmt->execute();
            $stmt->close();

            $notificationTitle = 'Application Received';
            $notificationMessage = 'Your quarter application has been received and is under review.';
            $notificationStmt = $conn->prepare("INSERT INTO notifications (nic, title, message, is_read) VALUES (?, ?, ?, 0)");
            $notificationStmt->bind_param("sss", $nic, $notificationTitle, $notificationMessage);
            $notificationStmt->execute();
            $notificationStmt->close();

            // Commit transaction
            $conn->commit();
            $_SESSION['success_message'] = "Application submitted successfully!";
            redirect('/dashboard');

        } catch (Exception $e) {
            $conn->rollback();
            $_SESSION['form_error'] = "Error submitting application: " . $e->getMessage();
            redirect($formPath);
        }
        $conn->close();
    }

    /**
     * Helper to handle file uploads
     */
    private function uploadFile($fieldName, $subDir) {
        if (isset($_FILES[$fieldName]) && $_FILES[$fieldName]['error'] === UPLOAD_ERR_OK) {
            $uploadDir = __DIR__ . '/../../public/uploads/' . $subDir . '/';
            if (!is_dir($uploadDir)) {
                mkdir($uploadDir, 0777, true);
            }
            $ext = pathinfo($_FILES[$fieldName]['name'], PATHINFO_EXTENSION);
            $filename = uniqid() . '.' . $ext;
            $dest = $uploadDir . $filename;
            if (move_uploaded_file($_FILES[$fieldName]['tmp_name'], $dest)) {
                return 'uploads/' . $subDir . '/' . $filename;
            }
        }
        return null;
    }
}
?>

File: src/controllers/ApprovalController.php

<?php
require_once __DIR__ . '/../models/Approval.php';
require_once __DIR__ . '/AuthController.php';

class ApprovalController {
    private $approvalModel;
    private $auth;

    public function __construct() {
        $this->auth = new AuthController();
        $this->auth->requireLogin();
        $this->approvalModel = new Approval();
    }

    public function submitClerk() {
        $applicationId = $this->getApplicationId();
        $yesNo = static function ($value) {
            return $value === 'Yes' ? 1 : ($value === 'No' ? 0 : null);
        };
        if (isset($_POST['is_another_residence_permanent'])) {
            $otherQuarter = $yesNo($_POST['is_another_residence_permanent']);
        } elseif (isset($_POST['is_not_another_residence_permanent'])) {
            $otherQuarter = $_POST['is_not_another_residence_permanent'] === 'No' ? 0 : null;
        } else {
            $otherQuarter = null;
        }
        $data = [
            'forfeited' => $yesNo($_POST['clerk_rec_forfeit'] ?? null),
            'expiry_date' => ($_POST['house_vacated_date'] ?? '') ?: null,
            'mihindu_recipient' => $yesNo($_POST['clerk_rec_waitlist'] ?? null),
            'other_quarter' => $otherQuarter,
            'remarks' => trim((string)($_POST['clerk_rec_other_notes'] ?? '')) ?: null,
            'cabinet_number' => trim((string)($_POST['clerk_reg_no'] ?? '')) ?: null
        ];
        $this->saveAndRedirect(
            $this->approvalModel->saveClerkRecommendation($applicationId, $data, $_SESSION['nic']),
            '/approval/clerk/en',
            $applicationId
        );
    }

    public function submitSuperintendent() {
        $applicationId = $this->getApplicationId();
        $data = [
            'quarter_type_number' => trim((string)($_POST['rec_quarter_type_no'] ?? '')) ?: null,
            'remarks' => trim((string)($_POST['rec_other_remarks'] ?? '')) ?: null,
            'approval_date' => ($_POST['rec_date'] ?? '') ?: null
        ];
        $this->saveAndRedirect(
            $this->approvalModel->saveSuperintendentRecommendation($applicationId, $data, $_SESSION['nic']),
            '/approval/deputy/en',
            $applicationId
        );
    }

    public function submitMarks() {
        $applicationId = $this->getApplicationId();
        $scores = [];
        for ($index = 1; $index <= 8; $index++) {
            $value = filter_var($_POST['score_' . $index] ?? 0, FILTER_VALIDATE_INT);
            if ($value === false || $value < 0) {
                throw new RuntimeException('Evaluation marks must be non-negative whole numbers.');
            }
            $scores[] = $value;
        }
        $this->saveAndRedirect(
            $this->approvalModel->saveEvaluationMarks($applicationId, $scores, $_SESSION['nic']),
            '/approval/marks',
            $applicationId
        );
    }

    private function getApplicationId() {
        $applicationId = filter_var($_POST['application_id'] ?? null, FILTER_VALIDATE_INT);
        if (!$applicationId || !$this->approvalModel->applicationExists($applicationId)) {
            throw new RuntimeException('A valid application_id is required for this approval.');
        }
        return $applicationId;
    }

    private function saveAndRedirect($saved, $path, $applicationId) {
        if (!$saved) {
            throw new RuntimeException('The approval could not be saved.');
        }
        $_SESSION['approval_message'] = 'Approval details saved.';
        redirect($path . '?application_id=' . $applicationId);
    }
}

================
File: src/controllers/AuthController.php
================
<?php
require_once __DIR__ . '/../models/User.php';
require_once __DIR__ . '/../../config/config.php';

class AuthController {
    private $userModel;
    private $maxLoginAttempts = 5;
    private $lockoutTime = 900; // 15 minutes in seconds
    
    public function __construct() {
        $this->userModel = new User();
        // Session is already started in index.php
        if (session_status() === PHP_SESSION_NONE) {
            session_start();
        }
        
        // Regenerate session ID periodically for security
        if (!isset($_SESSION['session_created'])) {
            session_regenerate_id(true);
            $_SESSION['session_created'] = time();
        } elseif (time() - $_SESSION['session_created'] > 1800) {
            // Regenerate session every 30 minutes
            session_regenerate_id(true);
            $_SESSION['session_created'] = time();
        }
    }
    
    /**
     * Check if user is logged in
     */
    public function isLoggedIn() {
        return isset($_SESSION['nic']) && isset($_SESSION['session_valid']);
    }
    
    /**
     * Get current user
     */
    public function getCurrentUser() {
        if ($this->isLoggedIn()) {
            return [
                'nic' => $_SESSION['nic'],
                'name' => $_SESSION['user_name'] ?? 'User',
                'email' => $_SESSION['user_email'] ?? '',
                'computer_number' => $_SESSION['computer_number'] ?? ''
            ];
        }
        return null;
    }
    
    /**
     * Require login middleware
     */
    public function requireLogin() {
        if (!$this->isLoggedIn()) {
            $_SESSION['redirect_after_login'] = $_SERVER['REQUEST_URI'];
            redirect('/login');
            exit();
        }
        
        // Validate session fingerprint
        if ($this->sessionFingerprintChanged()) {
            $this->logout();
            redirect('/login?error=session_expired');
            exit();
        }
    }
    
    /**
     * Session fingerprint for security
     */
    private function getSessionFingerprint() {
        return hash('sha256', $_SERVER['HTTP_USER_AGENT'] . $_SERVER['REMOTE_ADDR']);
    }
    
    /**
     * Check if session fingerprint has changed
     */
    private function sessionFingerprintChanged() {
        if (!isset($_SESSION['fingerprint'])) {
            return true;
        }
        return $_SESSION['fingerprint'] !== $this->getSessionFingerprint();
    }
    
    /**
     * Login user with security measures - FIXED VERSION
     */
    public function login($nic, $password) {
        // Sanitize input
        $nic = trim($nic);
        $password = trim($password);
        
        // Validate input
        if (empty($nic) || empty($password)) {
            $this->logAttempt($nic, false);
            return ['success' => false, 'message' => 'Please enter both NIC and Password'];
        }
        
        // Check for brute force
        $attempts = $this->userModel->getLoginAttempts($nic);
        if ($attempts >= $this->maxLoginAttempts) {
            return ['success' => false, 'message' => 'Too many login attempts. Please try again later.'];
        }
        
        // Verify credentials
        $user = $this->userModel->verifyLogin($nic, $password);
        
        if ($user) {
            // Login successful
            $_SESSION['nic'] = $user['nic'];
            $_SESSION['user_name'] = $user['name'];
            $_SESSION['user_email'] = $user['email'];
            $_SESSION['computer_number'] = $user['computer_number'] ?? '';
            $_SESSION['session_valid'] = true;
            $_SESSION['fingerprint'] = $this->getSessionFingerprint();
            $_SESSION['logged_in_at'] = time();
            
            // Regenerate session ID after login
            session_regenerate_id(true);
            
            // Log successful attempt
            $this->logAttempt($nic, true);
            
            return ['success' => true, 'message' => 'Login successful'];
        } else {
            // Login failed
            $this->logAttempt($nic, false);
            return ['success' => false, 'message' => 'Invalid NIC or Password'];
        }
    }
    
    /**
     * Log login attempt
     */
    private function logAttempt($nic, $success) {
        $ip = $_SERVER['REMOTE_ADDR'] ?? '0.0.0.0';
        $this->userModel->logLoginAttempt($nic, $ip, $success);
    }
    
    /**
     * Logout user
     */
    public function logout() {
        // Clear session
        $_SESSION = array();
        
        // Delete session cookie
        if (ini_get("session.use_cookies")) {
            $params = session_get_cookie_params();
            setcookie(session_name(), '', time() - 42000,
                $params["path"], $params["domain"],
                $params["secure"], $params["httponly"]
            );
        }
        
        session_destroy();
        
        // Redirect to login
        redirect('/login');
        exit();
    }
    
    /**
     * Register new user with validation
     */
    public function register($data) {
        // Validate data
        $validation = $this->validateRegistration($data);
        if (!$validation['valid']) {
            return ['success' => false, 'message' => $validation['message']];
        }
        
        // Check if NIC already exists
        if ($this->userModel->nicExists($data['nic'])) {
            return ['success' => false, 'message' => 'NIC already registered'];
        }
        
        // Check if email already exists
        if ($this->userModel->emailExists($data['email'])) {
            return ['success' => false, 'message' => 'Email already registered'];
        }
        
        // Check if computer number already exists
        if ($this->userModel->computerNumberExists($data['computer_number'])) {
            return ['success' => false, 'message' => 'Computer Number already registered'];
        }
        
        // Create user
        $result = $this->userModel->create($data);
        
        if ($result['success']) {
            return ['success' => true, 'message' => 'Registration successful! Please login.'];
        } else {
            return ['success' => false, 'message' => 'Registration failed: ' . ($result['error'] ?? 'Unknown error')];
        }
    }
    
    /**
     * Validate registration data
     */
    private function validateRegistration($data) {
        // Required fields
        $required = ['name', 'nic', 'email', 'computer_number', 'mobile', 'password', 'confirm_password'];
        foreach ($required as $field) {
            if (empty($data[$field])) {
                return ['valid' => false, 'message' => 'All fields are required'];
            }
        }
        
        // Password match
        if ($data['password'] !== $data['confirm_password']) {
            return ['valid' => false, 'message' => 'Passwords do not match'];
        }
        
        // Password strength
        $password = $data['password'];
        if (strlen($password) < 8) {
            return ['valid' => false, 'message' => 'Password must be at least 8 characters'];
        }
        if (!preg_match('/[A-Z]/', $password)) {
            return ['valid' => false, 'message' => 'Password must contain at least one uppercase letter'];
        }
        if (!preg_match('/[a-z]/', $password)) {
            return ['valid' => false, 'message' => 'Password must contain at least one lowercase letter'];
        }
        if (!preg_match('/[0-9]/', $password)) {
            return ['valid' => false, 'message' => 'Password must contain at least one number'];
        }
        
        // Email validation
        if (!filter_var($data['email'], FILTER_VALIDATE_EMAIL)) {
            return ['valid' => false, 'message' => 'Invalid email address'];
        }
        
        // NIC validation (Sri Lankan NIC format)
        $nic = $data['nic'];
        if (!preg_match('/^[0-9]{9,12}$/', $nic)) {
            return ['valid' => false, 'message' => 'Invalid NIC format. Must be 9-12 digits'];
        }
        
        // Mobile validation
        $mobile = $data['mobile'];
        if (!preg_match('/^[0-9]{10}$/', $mobile)) {
            return ['valid' => false, 'message' => 'Invalid mobile number. Must be 10 digits'];
        }
        
        return ['valid' => true];
    }
}

<?php
/**
 * Complaint Controller
 */
require_once __DIR__ . '/../models/Complaint.php';

class ComplaintController {
    
    private $complaintModel;

    public function __construct() {
        $this->complaintModel = new Complaint();
    }

    public function store() {
        if ($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] === 'POST') {
            // 1. Get form data
            $complaints = $_POST['complaints'] ?? '';
            $related_issue = $_POST['related_issue'] ?? '';
            
            // If 'other' is selected, use custom_issue
            if ($related_issue === 'other' && !empty($_POST['custom_issue'])) {
                $related_issue = trim($_POST['custom_issue']);
            }

            // Get logged-in user's NIC
            $applicantNic = $_SESSION['nic'] ?? null;
            $this->complaintModel->create($applicantNic, $complaints, $related_issue);

            // Redirect to dashboard
            header("Location: " . baseUrl('dashboard'));
            exit();
        }
    }
}

<?php
require_once __DIR__ . '/../models/Notification.php';
require_once __DIR__ . '/AuthController.php';

class NotificationController {
    private $auth;
    private $notificationModel;
    
    public function __construct() {
        $this->auth = new AuthController();
        $this->auth->requireLogin();
        $this->notificationModel = new Notification();
    }
    
    /**
     * Get all notifications for the logged-in user
     */
    public function getAll() {
        $nic = $_SESSION['nic'] ?? null;
        if (!$nic) {
            return [];
        }
        return $this->notificationModel->getByNic($nic);
    }
    
    /**
     * Get unread count for the dashboard badge
     */
    public function getUnreadCount() {
        $nic = $_SESSION['nic'] ?? null;
        if (!$nic) {
            return 0;
        }
        return $this->notificationModel->getUnreadCount($nic);
    }
    
    /**
     * Mark notification as read (AJAX)
     */
    public function markAsRead($id) {
        $nic = $_SESSION['nic'] ?? null;
        if (!$nic) {
            return ['status' => 'error', 'success' => false, 'message' => 'Unauthorized session'];
        }
        
        $result = $this->notificationModel->markAsRead($id, $nic);
        
        if ($result) {
            return ['status' => 'success', 'success' => true, 'message' => '✅ Notification marked as read!'];
        }
        return ['status' => 'error', 'success' => false, 'message' => 'Failed to mark as read'];
    }
    
    /**
     * Delete notification (AJAX)
     */
    public function delete($id) {
        $nic = $_SESSION['nic'] ?? null;
        if (!$nic) {
            return ['status' => 'error', 'success' => false, 'message' => 'Unauthorized session'];
        }
        
        $result = $this->notificationModel->delete($id, $nic);
        
        if ($result) {
            return ['status' => 'success', 'success' => true, 'message' => '🗑️ Notification deleted!'];
        }
        return ['status' => 'error', 'success' => false, 'message' => 'Failed to delete notification'];
    }
}

<?php
require_once __DIR__ . '/../models/Offer.php';
require_once __DIR__ . '/AuthController.php';

class OfferController {
    private $auth;
    private $offerModel;

    public function __construct() {
        $this->auth = new AuthController();
        $this->auth->requireLogin();
        $this->offerModel = new Offer();
    }

    public function getOffers() {
        return $this->offerModel->getByNic($_SESSION['nic']);
    }

    public function respond() {
        $id = filter_var($_POST['offer_id'] ?? null, FILTER_VALIDATE_INT);
        $action = $_POST['response'] ?? '';
        if (!$id || !in_array($action, ['accept', 'postpone', 'deny'], true)) {
            $_SESSION['offer_message'] = 'Choose a valid offer response.';
        } else {
            $nic = $_SESSION['nic'];
            $saved = match ($action) {
                'accept' => $this->offerModel->accept($id, $nic),
                'postpone' => $this->offerModel->postpone($id, $nic),
                'deny' => $this->offerModel->deny($id, $nic)
            };
            $_SESSION['offer_message'] = $saved ? 'Your offer response was saved.' : 'The offer could not be updated.';
        }
        redirect('/offer/respond');
    }
}

<?php
require_once __DIR__ . '/../models/Application.php';
require_once __DIR__ . '/AuthController.php';

class PreferenceController {
    private $auth;
    private $applicationModel;

    public function __construct() {
        $this->auth = new AuthController();
        $this->auth->requireLogin();
        $this->applicationModel = new Application();
    }

    /**
     * Save residence type (called when user clicks
     * "Open Joint Residence" or "Open Application Form")
     */
    public function saveResidenceType() {
        $nic = $_SESSION['nic'];
        $residenceType = $_POST['residence_type'] ?? '';

        // Matches the enum in your DB: 'single' | 'joint_residence'
        if (!in_array($residenceType, ['single', 'joint_residence'], true)) {
            $_SESSION['preference_error'] = 'Invalid residence type.';
            redirect('/application/residence');
            return;
        }

        $this->applicationModel->savePreference($nic, $residenceType);
        $_SESSION['residence_type'] = $residenceType;

        // Redirect based on residence type
        if ($residenceType === 'joint_residence') {
            redirect('/application/apply.php?tab=joint');
        } else {
            redirect('/application/select-language?type=single');
        }
    }

    /**
     * Save language preference (called when user clicks Sinhala or English)
     * Matches the enum in your DB: 'en' | 'single_residence'
     */
    public function saveLanguage() {
        $nic = $_SESSION['nic'];
        $language = $_POST['language'] ?? '';
        $residenceType = $_POST['residence_type'] ?? 'single';

        if (!in_array($residenceType, ['single', 'joint_residence'], true)) {
            $residenceType = 'single';
        }

        if (!in_array($language, ['en', 'single_residence'], true)) {
            $_SESSION['preference_error'] = 'Invalid language.';
            redirect('/application/select-language?type=' . urlencode($residenceType));
            return;
        }

        // Save residence type + language together
        $this->applicationModel->savePreference($nic, $residenceType, $language);
        $_SESSION['residence_type'] = $residenceType;
        $_SESSION['form_language'] = $language;

        // Redirect to the appropriate form
        // 'single_residence' = Sinhala form (per your enum naming)
        if ($language === 'single_residence') {
            redirect('/application/form/si?type=' . urlencode($residenceType));
        } else {
            redirect('/application/form/en?type=' . urlencode($residenceType));
        }
    }
}

<?php
require_once __DIR__ . '/../models/User.php';
require_once __DIR__ . '/AuthController.php';

class ProfileController {
    private $auth;
    private $userModel;
    
    public function __construct() {
        $this->auth = new AuthController();
        $this->auth->requireLogin();
        $this->userModel = new User();
    }
    
    /**
     * Get user profile
     */
    public function getProfile() {
        $nic = $_SESSION['nic'];
        return $this->userModel->findByNic($nic);
    }
    
    /**
     * Update full profile with all fields
     */
    public function updateProfile($name, $nic, $email, $computer_no, $mobile, $password = null) {
        $currentNic = $_SESSION['nic'];
        
        // Check if NIC is being changed and if it already exists
        if ($nic !== $currentNic && $this->userModel->nicExists($nic)) {
            return ['success' => false, 'message' => 'NIC already registered to another user'];
        }
        
        // Check if email already exists (if changed)
        $userData = $this->userModel->findByNic($currentNic);
        if ($email !== $userData['email'] && $this->userModel->emailExists($email)) {
            return ['success' => false, 'message' => 'Email already registered to another user'];
        }
        
        // Check if computer number already exists (if changed)
        if ($computer_no !== $userData['computer_number'] && $this->userModel->computerNumberExists($computer_no)) {
            return ['success' => false, 'message' => 'Computer Number already registered to another user'];
        }
        
        // Update the profile
        $result = $this->userModel->updateFullProfile($currentNic, $name, $nic, $email, $computer_no, $mobile, $password);
        
        if ($result) {
            // Update session if NIC changed
            if ($nic !== $currentNic) {
                $_SESSION['nic'] = $nic;
            }
            $_SESSION['user_name'] = $name;
            $_SESSION['user_email'] = $email;
            $_SESSION['computer_number'] = $computer_no;
            
            return ['success' => true, 'message' => 'Profile updated successfully!'];
        }
        
        return ['success' => false, 'message' => 'Error updating profile'];
    }
}
?>

File: src/models/Application.php
