<?php
require_once __DIR__ . '/../../config/config.php';

class Application {
    private $conn;
    private $table = 'applications';
    
    public function __construct() {
        $this->conn = getDBConnection();
    }
    
    /**
     * Create new application
     */
    public function create($nic, $quarterType) {
        $userStmt = $this->conn->prepare("SELECT computer_number FROM users WHERE nic = ? AND is_active = 1");
        $userStmt->bind_param("s", $nic);
        $userStmt->execute();
        $user = $userStmt->get_result()->fetch_assoc();
        $userStmt->close();

        if (!$user || empty($user['computer_number'])) {
            return ['success' => false, 'error' => 'A registered computer number is required.'];
        }

        $computerNo = $user['computer_number'];
        $applicationDate = date('Y-m-d');
        
        $sql = "INSERT INTO {$this->table} (
                nic, computer_no, quarter_type, application_date, status,
                application_status, submit_status, application_submitted_at
            ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, 'pending', 'submitted', 'success', NOW())";
        $stmt = $this->conn->prepare($sql);
        $stmt->bind_param("ssss", $nic, $computerNo, $quarterType, $applicationDate);
        
        if ($stmt->execute()) {
            return [
                'success' => true,
                'computer_no' => $computerNo,
                'application_id' => $stmt->insert_id
            ];
        }
        return ['success' => false, 'error' => $this->conn->error];
    }
    
    /**
     * Get application by NIC
     */
    public function findByNic($nic) {
        $sql = "SELECT * FROM {$this->table} WHERE nic = ? ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1";
        $stmt = $this->conn->prepare($sql);
        $stmt->bind_param("s", $nic);
        $stmt->execute();
        $result = $stmt->get_result();
        return $result->fetch_assoc();
    }
    
    /**
     * Get application by computer number ONLY (for admin/search without NIC)
     */
    public function findByComputerNo($computerNo) {
        $sql = "SELECT * FROM {$this->table} WHERE computer_no = ? ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1";
        $stmt = $this->conn->prepare($sql);
        $stmt->bind_param("s", $computerNo);
        $stmt->execute();
        $result = $stmt->get_result();
        return $result->fetch_assoc();
    }
    
    /**
     * Get application by computer number AND NIC (for user-specific search)
     */
    public function findByComputerNoAndNic($computerNo, $nic) {
        $sql = "SELECT * FROM {$this->table} WHERE computer_no = ? AND nic = ? ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1";
        $stmt = $this->conn->prepare($sql);
        $stmt->bind_param("ss", $computerNo, $nic);
        $stmt->execute();
        $result = $stmt->get_result();
        return $result->fetch_assoc();
    }
    
    /**
     * Get application with waiting list position
     */
    public function getApplicationWithPosition($nic) {
        $application = $this->findByNic($nic);
        if (!$application) {
            return null;
        }
        
        // Get waiting list position
          $pos_sql = "SELECT position FROM waiting_list WHERE nic = ? AND quarter_type = ? ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1";
        
        $pos_stmt = $this->conn->prepare($pos_sql);
          $pos_stmt->bind_param("ss", $nic, $application['quarter_type']);
        $pos_stmt->execute();
        $pos_result = $pos_stmt->get_result();
        $position = null;
        
        if ($pos_row = $pos_result->fetch_assoc()) {
            $position = $pos_row['position'];
        }
        
        $application['position'] = $position;
        return $application;
    }
    /** Save or update the current user's latest draft preference. */
    public function savePreference($nic, $residenceType, $formLanguage = null) {
        $stmt = $this->conn->prepare(
            "SELECT id FROM {$this->table} WHERE nic = ? AND application_status = 'draft' ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1"
        );
        $stmt->bind_param("s", $nic);
        $stmt->execute();
        $existing = $stmt->get_result()->fetch_assoc();
        $stmt->close();

        if ($existing) {
            if ($formLanguage !== null) {
                $sql = "UPDATE {$this->table} SET residence_type = ?, form_language = ? WHERE id = ?";
                $stmt = $this->conn->prepare($sql);
                $stmt->bind_param("ssi", $residenceType, $formLanguage, $existing['id']);
            } else {
                $sql = "UPDATE {$this->table} SET residence_type = ? WHERE id = ?";
                $stmt = $this->conn->prepare($sql);
                $stmt->bind_param("si", $residenceType, $existing['id']);
            }
            $stmt->execute();
            $stmt->close();
            return $existing['id'];
        }

        $computerNo = '';
        $userStmt = $this->conn->prepare(
            "SELECT computer_number FROM users WHERE nic = ? AND is_active = 1"
        );
        $userStmt->bind_param("s", $nic);
        $userStmt->execute();
        $userRow = $userStmt->get_result()->fetch_assoc();
        $userStmt->close();
        if ($userRow && !empty($userRow['computer_number'])) {
            $computerNo = $userRow['computer_number'];
        }

        $sql = "INSERT INTO {$this->table} (
                    nic, computer_no, quarter_type, application_date,
                    residence_type, form_language, application_status, status, submit_status
                ) VALUES (?, ?, '', CURDATE(), ?, ?, 'draft', 'pending', 'pending')";
        $stmt = $this->conn->prepare($sql);
        $stmt->bind_param("ssss", $nic, $computerNo, $residenceType, $formLanguage);
        $stmt->execute();
        $newId = $stmt->insert_id;
        $stmt->close();
        return $newId;
    }

    /** Return the latest draft preference for a user, if one exists. */
    public function getPreference($nic) {
        $sql = "SELECT id, residence_type, form_language FROM {$this->table}
                WHERE nic = ? AND application_status = 'draft' ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1";
        $stmt = $this->conn->prepare($sql);
        $stmt->bind_param("s", $nic);
        $stmt->execute();
        $row = $stmt->get_result()->fetch_assoc();
        $stmt->close();
        return $row ?: null;
    }
    public function __destruct() {
        if ($this->conn) {
            $this->conn->close();
        }
    }
}
?>

<?php
require_once __DIR__ . '/../../config/config.php';

class Approval {
    private $conn;

    public function __construct() {
        $this->conn = getDBConnection();
    }

    public function saveClerkRecommendation($applicationId, $data, $nic) {
        $sql = "INSERT INTO clerk_recommendations (
                    application_id, clerk_is_right_forfeited, clerk_forfeit_expiry_date,
                    clerk_is_mihindusethpura_recipient, clerk_residing_in_other_gov_quarter,
                    clerk_other_remarks, clerk_cabinet_number, clerk_recommendation_status,
                    clerk_recommended_at, clerk_signed_by_nic, clerk_signed_at
                ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 'recommended', NOW(), ?, NOW())
                ON DUPLICATE KEY UPDATE
                    clerk_is_right_forfeited = VALUES(clerk_is_right_forfeited),
                    clerk_forfeit_expiry_date = VALUES(clerk_forfeit_expiry_date),
                    clerk_is_mihindusethpura_recipient = VALUES(clerk_is_mihindusethpura_recipient),
                    clerk_residing_in_other_gov_quarter = VALUES(clerk_residing_in_other_gov_quarter),
                    clerk_other_remarks = VALUES(clerk_other_remarks),
                    clerk_cabinet_number = VALUES(clerk_cabinet_number),
                    clerk_recommendation_status = 'recommended', clerk_recommended_at = NOW(),
                    clerk_signed_by_nic = VALUES(clerk_signed_by_nic), clerk_signed_at = NOW()";
        $stmt = $this->conn->prepare($sql);
        $stmt->bind_param(
            'issiisss',
            $applicationId,
            $data['forfeited'],
            $data['expiry_date'],
            $data['mihindu_recipient'],
            $data['other_quarter'],
            $data['remarks'],
            $data['cabinet_number'],
            $nic
        );
        $result = $stmt->execute();
        $stmt->close();
        if (!$result) {
            return false;
        }

        $statusStmt = $this->conn->prepare("UPDATE applications SET clerk_status = 'approved', application_status = 'pending_super' WHERE id = ?");
        $statusStmt->bind_param('i', $applicationId);
        $result = $statusStmt->execute();
        $statusStmt->close();
        return $result;
    }

    public function saveSuperintendentRecommendation($applicationId, $data, $nic) {
        $sql = "INSERT INTO superintendent_recommendations (
                    application_id, super_quarter_type_and_number,
                    super_essentiality_and_remarks, super_approval_date,
                    super_recommendation_status, super_recommended_at,
                    super_signed_by_nic, super_signed_at
                ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, 'recommended', NOW(), ?, NOW())
                ON DUPLICATE KEY UPDATE
                    super_quarter_type_and_number = VALUES(super_quarter_type_and_number),
                    super_essentiality_and_remarks = VALUES(super_essentiality_and_remarks),
                    super_approval_date = VALUES(super_approval_date),
                    super_recommendation_status = 'recommended', super_recommended_at = NOW(),
                    super_signed_by_nic = VALUES(super_signed_by_nic), super_signed_at = NOW()";
        $stmt = $this->conn->prepare($sql);
        $stmt->bind_param(
            'issss',
            $applicationId,
            $data['quarter_type_number'],
            $data['remarks'],
            $data['approval_date'],
            $nic
        );
        $result = $stmt->execute();
        $stmt->close();
        if (!$result) {
            return false;
        }

        $statusStmt = $this->conn->prepare("UPDATE applications SET branch_status = 'approved', application_status = 'approved' WHERE id = ?");
        $statusStmt->bind_param('i', $applicationId);
        $result = $statusStmt->execute();
        $statusStmt->close();
        return $result;
    }

    public function saveEvaluationMarks($applicationId, $scores, $nic) {
        $total = array_sum($scores);
        $sql = "INSERT INTO evaluation_marks (
                    application_id, mark_waiting_list_duration, mark_child_count,
                    mark_housing_condition, mark_distance_above_30km,
                    mark_applicant_serious_illness, mark_dependent_serious_illness,
                    mark_marital_status, mark_tie_breaker_service_years,
                    total_evaluation_marks, marks_evaluated_by_nic
                ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
                ON DUPLICATE KEY UPDATE
                    mark_waiting_list_duration = VALUES(mark_waiting_list_duration),
                    mark_child_count = VALUES(mark_child_count),
                    mark_housing_condition = VALUES(mark_housing_condition),
                    mark_distance_above_30km = VALUES(mark_distance_above_30km),
                    mark_applicant_serious_illness = VALUES(mark_applicant_serious_illness),
                    mark_dependent_serious_illness = VALUES(mark_dependent_serious_illness),
                    mark_marital_status = VALUES(mark_marital_status),
                    mark_tie_breaker_service_years = VALUES(mark_tie_breaker_service_years),
                    total_evaluation_marks = VALUES(total_evaluation_marks),
                    marks_evaluated_at = NOW(), marks_evaluated_by_nic = VALUES(marks_evaluated_by_nic)";
        $stmt = $this->conn->prepare($sql);
        $stmt->bind_param(
            'iiiiiiiiiis',
            $applicationId,
            $scores[0], $scores[1], $scores[2], $scores[3],
            $scores[4], $scores[5], $scores[6], $scores[7],
            $total, $nic
        );
        $result = $stmt->execute();
        $stmt->close();
        if (!$result) {
            return false;
        }

        $applicationStmt = $this->conn->prepare('UPDATE applications SET marks = ? WHERE id = ?');
        $applicationStmt->bind_param('ii', $total, $applicationId);
        $result = $applicationStmt->execute();
        $applicationStmt->close();
        if (!$result) {
            return false;
        }

        $waitingStmt = $this->conn->prepare("UPDATE waiting_list SET employee_marks = ? WHERE nic = (SELECT nic FROM applications WHERE id = ?) ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1");
        $waitingStmt->bind_param('ii', $total, $applicationId);
        $result = $waitingStmt->execute();
        $waitingStmt->close();
        return $result;
    }

    public function applicationExists($applicationId) {
        $stmt = $this->conn->prepare('SELECT id FROM applications WHERE id = ?');
        $stmt->bind_param('i', $applicationId);
        $stmt->execute();
        $exists = $stmt->get_result()->num_rows === 1;
        $stmt->close();
        return $exists;
    }

    public function __destruct() {
        if ($this->conn) {
            $this->conn->close();
        }
    }
}

<?php
require_once __DIR__ . '/../../config/config.php';

class Complaint {
    private $conn;

    public function __construct() {
        $this->conn = getDBConnection();
    }

    public function create($nic, $details, $relatedIssue) {
        $stmt = $this->conn->prepare("INSERT INTO complaints (user_id, complaints, related_issue, status) VALUES (?, ?, ?, 'Pending')");
        $stmt->bind_param('sss', $nic, $details, $relatedIssue);
        $saved = $stmt->execute();
        $complaintId = $stmt->insert_id;
        $stmt->close();

        if (!$saved) {
            return false;
        }

        $notificationMessage = 'New complaint received regarding: ' . $relatedIssue;
        $notificationStmt = $this->conn->prepare("INSERT INTO notifications (nic, title, message, is_read) SELECT nic, 'New Complaint', ?, 0 FROM users WHERE role = 'subject_clerk'");
        $notificationStmt->bind_param('s', $notificationMessage);
        $notificationStmt->execute();
        $notificationStmt->close();

        return $complaintId;
    }

    public function __destruct() {
        if ($this->conn) {
            $this->conn->close();
        }
    }
}

<?php
require_once __DIR__ . '/../../config/config.php';

class DropdownModel {
    private $conn;

    public function __construct() {
        $this->conn = getDBConnection();
    }

    /**
     * Shared helper for the simple lookup tables.
     * Returns rows as: id, name (label in the chosen language), value (English name stored in the DB).
     * Table and column names are fixed in the methods below - never pass user input here.
     */
    private function getLookup($table, $column, $columnSi, $language = 'en') {
        $langField = $language === 'si' ? $columnSi : $column;
        $sql = "SELECT id, {$langField} AS name, {$column} AS value
                FROM {$table} WHERE is_active = 1 ORDER BY sort_order ASC";
        $result = $this->conn->query($sql);
        return $result->fetch_all(MYSQLI_ASSOC);
    }

    /**
     * Get all active quarter types
     */
    public function getQuarterTypes($language = 'en') {
        return $this->getLookup('quarter_types', 'type_name', 'type_name_si', $language);
    }

    /**
     * Get all active designations
     */
    public function getDesignations($language = 'en') {
        return $this->getLookup('designations', 'designation_name', 'designation_name_si', $language);
    }

    /**
     * Get all active sub-departments
     */
    public function getSubDepartments($language = 'en') {
        return $this->getLookup('sub_departments', 'dept_name', 'dept_name_si', $language);
    }

    /**
     * Get all active districts
     */
    public function getDistricts($language = 'en') {
        return $this->getLookup('districts', 'district_name', 'district_name_si', $language);
    }

    /**
     * Get all active quarter locations (applied_quarter_location)
     */
    public function getQuarterLocations($language = 'en') {
        return $this->getLookup('quarter_locations', 'location_name', 'location_name_si', $language);
    }

    /**
     * Get all active work stations (applicant_current_work_station)
     */
    public function getWorkStations($language = 'en') {
        return $this->getLookup('work_stations', 'station_name', 'station_name_si', $language);
    }

    /**
     * Get all active salary account branches (applicant_salary_account_branch)
     */
    public function getSalaryAccountBranches($language = 'en') {
        return $this->getLookup('salary_account_branches', 'branch_name', 'branch_name_si', $language);
    }

    /**
     * Get all active personal file offices (applicant_personal_file_office)
     */
    public function getPersonalFileOffices($language = 'en') {
        return $this->getLookup('personal_file_offices', 'office_name', 'office_name_si', $language);
    }

    /**
     * Get all active quarters (applied_quarter_number). The form filters them
     * by the selected location in JavaScript.
     */
    public function getQuarters() {
        $sql = "SELECT quarter_number, location_name, quarter_type
                FROM quarters WHERE is_active = 1
                ORDER BY sort_order ASC, quarter_number ASC";
        $result = $this->conn->query($sql);
        return $result->fetch_all(MYSQLI_ASSOC);
    }

    /**
     * Get all active quarter numbers (applied_quarter_number dropdown)
     */
    public function getQuarterNumbers($language = 'en') {
        $sql = "SELECT id, quarter_number AS name, quarter_number AS value
                FROM quarter_numbers WHERE is_active = 1 ORDER BY sort_order ASC";
        $result = $this->conn->query($sql);
        return $result->fetch_all(MYSQLI_ASSOC);
    }

    public function __destruct() {
        if ($this->conn) {
            $this->conn->close();
        }
    }
}

require_once __DIR__ . '/../../config/config.php';

class Notification {
    private $conn;
    private $table = 'notifications';
    
    public function __construct() {
        $this->conn = getDBConnection();
    }
    
    /**
     * Create notification
     */
    public function create($nic, $title, $message) {
        $sql = "INSERT INTO {$this->table} (nic, title, message, is_read) VALUES (?, ?, ?, 0)";
        $stmt = $this->conn->prepare($sql);
        $stmt->bind_param("sss", $nic, $title, $message);
        return $stmt->execute();
    }
    
    /**
     * Get all notifications for user
     */
    public function getByNic($nic) {
        $sql = "SELECT * FROM {$this->table} WHERE nic = ? ORDER BY id DESC";
        $stmt = $this->conn->prepare($sql);
        $stmt->bind_param("s", $nic);
        $stmt->execute();
        $result = $stmt->get_result();
        return $result->fetch_all(MYSQLI_ASSOC);
    }
    
    /**
     * Get unread count
     */
    public function getUnreadCount($nic) {
        $sql = "SELECT COUNT(*) as count FROM {$this->table} WHERE nic = ? AND is_read = 0";
        $stmt = $this->conn->prepare($sql);
        $stmt->bind_param("s", $nic);
        $stmt->execute();
        $result = $stmt->get_result();
        $row = $result->fetch_assoc();
        return $row['count'] ?? 0;
    }
    
    /**
     * Mark as read
     */
    public function markAsRead($id, $nic) {
        $sql = "UPDATE {$this->table} SET is_read = 1 WHERE id = ? AND nic = ?";
        $stmt = $this->conn->prepare($sql);
        $stmt->bind_param("is", $id, $nic);
        return $stmt->execute();
    }
    
    /**
     * Delete notification
     */
    public function delete($id, $nic) {
        $sql = "DELETE FROM {$this->table} WHERE id = ? AND nic = ?";
        $stmt = $this->conn->prepare($sql);
        $stmt->bind_param("is", $id, $nic);
        return $stmt->execute();
    }
    
    public function __destruct() {
        if ($this->conn) {
            $this->conn->close();
        }
    }
}

<?php
require_once __DIR__ . '/../../config/config.php';

class Offer {
    private $conn;
    private $table = 'respond_to_offer';
    
    public function __construct() {
        $this->conn = getDBConnection();
    }
    
    /**
     * Get active offer for user
     */
    public function getActiveOffer($nic) {
        $sql = "SELECT * FROM {$this->table} 
                WHERE nic = ? AND status IN ('approved', 'accepted') 
                ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT 1";
        $stmt = $this->conn->prepare($sql);
        $stmt->bind_param("s", $nic);
        $stmt->execute();
        $result = $stmt->get_result();
        return $result->fetch_assoc();
    }

    public function getByNic($nic) {
        $sql = "SELECT * FROM {$this->table} WHERE nic = ? ORDER BY created_at DESC, id DESC";
        $stmt = $this->conn->prepare($sql);
        $stmt->bind_param("s", $nic);
        $stmt->execute();
        $offers = $stmt->get_result()->fetch_all(MYSQLI_ASSOC);
        $stmt->close();
        return $offers;
    }
    
    /**
     * Accept offer
     */
    public function accept($id, $nic) {
        $sql = "UPDATE {$this->table} SET status = 'accepted' WHERE id = ? AND nic = ?";
        $stmt = $this->conn->prepare($sql);
        $stmt->bind_param("is", $id, $nic);
        $saved = $stmt->execute() && $stmt->affected_rows > 0;
        $stmt->close();
        return $saved;
    }
    
    /**
     * Postpone offer
     */
    public function postpone($id, $nic) {
        $sql = "UPDATE {$this->table} SET created_at = NOW(), status = 'pending' WHERE id = ? AND nic = ?";
        $stmt = $this->conn->prepare($sql);
        $stmt->bind_param("is", $id, $nic);
        $saved = $stmt->execute() && $stmt->affected_rows > 0;
        $stmt->close();
        return $saved;
    }
    
    /**
     * Deny offer
     */
    public function deny($id, $nic) {
        $sql = "DELETE FROM {$this->table} WHERE id = ? AND nic = ?";
        $stmt = $this->conn->prepare($sql);
        $stmt->bind_param("is", $id, $nic);
        $saved = $stmt->execute() && $stmt->affected_rows > 0;
        $stmt->close();
        return $saved;
    }
    
    public function __destruct() {
        if ($this->conn) {
            $this->conn->close();
        }
    }
}

File: src/models/User.php

<?php
require_once __DIR__ . '/../../config/config.php';

class User {
    private $conn;
    private $table = 'users';
    
    public function __construct() {
        $this->conn = getDBConnection();
    }
    
    /**
     * Find user by NIC
     */
    public function findByNic($nic) {
        $sql = "SELECT * FROM {$this->table} WHERE nic = ? AND is_active = 1";
        $stmt = $this->conn->prepare($sql);
        $stmt->bind_param("s", $nic);
        $stmt->execute();
        $result = $stmt->get_result();
        return $result->fetch_assoc();
    }
    
    /**
     * Find user by email
     */
    public function findByEmail($email) {
        $sql = "SELECT * FROM {$this->table} WHERE email = ? AND is_active = 1";
        $stmt = $this->conn->prepare($sql);
        $stmt->bind_param("s", $email);
        $stmt->execute();
        $result = $stmt->get_result();
        return $result->fetch_assoc();
    }
    
    /**
     * Check if NIC exists
     */
    public function nicExists($nic) {
        $sql = "SELECT COUNT(*) as count FROM {$this->table} WHERE nic = ?";
        $stmt = $this->conn->prepare($sql);
        $stmt->bind_param("s", $nic);
        $stmt->execute();
        $result = $stmt->get_result();
        $row = $result->fetch_assoc();
        return $row['count'] > 0;
    }
    
    /**
     * Check if email exists
     */
    public function emailExists($email) {
        $sql = "SELECT COUNT(*) as count FROM {$this->table} WHERE email = ?";
        $stmt = $this->conn->prepare($sql);
        $stmt->bind_param("s", $email);
        $stmt->execute();
        $result = $stmt->get_result();
        $row = $result->fetch_assoc();
        return $row['count'] > 0;
    }
    
    /**
     * Check if computer number exists
     */
    public function computerNumberExists($computerNumber) {
        $sql = "SELECT COUNT(*) as count FROM {$this->table} WHERE computer_number = ?";
        $stmt = $this->conn->prepare($sql);
        $stmt->bind_param("s", $computerNumber);
        $stmt->execute();
        $result = $stmt->get_result();
        $row = $result->fetch_assoc();
        return $row['count'] > 0;
    }
    
    /**
     * Create new user with bcrypt password
     */
    public function create($data) {
        // Hash password with bcrypt (cost factor 12)
        $hashedPassword = password_hash($data['password'], PASSWORD_BCRYPT, ['cost' => 12]);
        
        $sql = "INSERT INTO {$this->table} (nic, name, email, computer_number, mobile, password) 
                VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)";
        $stmt = $this->conn->prepare($sql);
        $stmt->bind_param("ssssss", 
            $data['nic'], 
            $data['name'], 
            $data['email'], 
            $data['computer_number'], 
            $data['mobile'], 
            $hashedPassword
        );
        
        if ($stmt->execute()) {
            return [
                'success' => true,
                'id' => $stmt->insert_id
            ];
        }
        return [
            'success' => false, 
            'error' => $this->conn->error
        ];
    }
    
    /**
     * Verify login credentials using bcrypt
     */
    public function verifyLogin($nic, $password) {
        $user = $this->findByNic($nic);
        
        if (!$user) {
            return false;
        }
        
        // Verify password using bcrypt
        if (password_verify($password, $user['password'])) {
            // Check if password needs rehashing
            if (password_needs_rehash($user['password'], PASSWORD_BCRYPT, ['cost' => 12])) {
                $newHash = password_hash($password, PASSWORD_BCRYPT, ['cost' => 12]);
                $this->updatePassword($nic, $newHash);
            }
            return $user;
        }
        
        return false;
    }
    
    /**
     * Update user password
     */
    public function updatePassword($nic, $hashedPassword) {
        $sql = "UPDATE {$this->table} SET password = ? WHERE nic = ?";
        $stmt = $this->conn->prepare($sql);
        $stmt->bind_param("ss", $hashedPassword, $nic);
        return $stmt->execute();
    }
    
    /**
     * Update user profile (simple version - keeps existing signature)
     */
    public function updateProfile($nic, $name, $email, $mobile = null) {
        $sql = "UPDATE {$this->table} SET name = ?, email = ?" . 
               ($mobile ? ", mobile = ?" : "") . 
               " WHERE nic = ?";
        
        if ($mobile) {
            $stmt = $this->conn->prepare($sql);
            $stmt->bind_param("ssss", $name, $email, $mobile, $nic);
        } else {
            $stmt = $this->conn->prepare($sql);
            $stmt->bind_param("sss", $name, $email, $nic);
        }
        
        return $stmt->execute();
    }
    
    /**
     * NEW: Update full user profile with all fields including password
     * This method is called by the enhanced edit_profile.php
     */
    public function updateFullProfile($currentNic, $name, $nic, $email, $computer_no, $mobile, $password = null) {
        if ($password && !empty($password)) {
            // With password update
            $hashedPassword = password_hash($password, PASSWORD_BCRYPT, ['cost' => 12]);
            $sql = "UPDATE {$this->table} SET 
                    name = ?, 
                    nic = ?, 
                    email = ?, 
                    computer_number = ?, 
                    mobile = ?, 
                    password = ? 
                    WHERE nic = ?";
            $stmt = $this->conn->prepare($sql);
            $stmt->bind_param("sssssss", $name, $nic, $email, $computer_no, $mobile, $hashedPassword, $currentNic);
        } else {
            // Without password update
            $sql = "UPDATE {$this->table} SET 
                    name = ?, 
                    nic = ?, 
                    email = ?, 
                    computer_number = ?, 
                    mobile = ? 
                    WHERE nic = ?";
            $stmt = $this->conn->prepare($sql);
            $stmt->bind_param("ssssss", $name, $nic, $email, $computer_no, $mobile, $currentNic);
        }
        
        return $stmt->execute();
    }
    
    /**
     * Rate limit login attempts
     */
    public function getLoginAttempts($nic) {
        $sql = "SELECT COUNT(*) as count FROM login_attempts 
                WHERE nic = ? AND attempt_time > DATE_SUB(NOW(), INTERVAL 15 MINUTE)";
        $stmt = $this->conn->prepare($sql);
        $stmt->bind_param("s", $nic);
        $stmt->execute();
        $result = $stmt->get_result();
        $row = $result->fetch_assoc();
        return $row['count'] ?? 0;
    }
    
    /**
     * Log login attempt
     */
    public function logLoginAttempt($nic, $ip, $success) {
        // Create login_attempts table if not exists
        $this->createLoginAttemptsTable();
        
        $sql = "INSERT INTO login_attempts (nic, ip_address, success, attempt_time) 
                VALUES (?, ?, ?, NOW())";
        $stmt = $this->conn->prepare($sql);
        $stmt->bind_param("ssi", $nic, $ip, $success);
        return $stmt->execute();
    }
    
    /**
     * Create login attempts table
     */
    private function createLoginAttemptsTable() {
        $sql = "CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS login_attempts (
            id INT AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
            nic VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
            ip_address VARCHAR(45) NOT NULL,
            success TINYINT(1) DEFAULT 0,
            attempt_time TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
            INDEX idx_nic_time (nic, attempt_time)
        )";
        $this->conn->query($sql);
    }
    
    public function __destruct() {
        if ($this->conn) {
            $this->conn->close();
        }
    }
}
?>

================
File: src/models/WaitingList.php
================
<?php
require_once __DIR__ . '/../../config/config.php';

class WaitingList {
    private $conn;
    private $table = 'waiting_list';
    
    public function __construct() {
        $this->conn = getDBConnection();
    }
    
    /**
     * Add applicant to waiting list
     */
    public function add($nic, $quarterType, $position) {
        $sql = "INSERT INTO {$this->table} (nic, position, quarter_type, applied_date) VALUES (?, ?, ?, CURDATE())";
        $stmt = $this->conn->prepare($sql);
        $stmt->bind_param("sis", $nic, $position, $quarterType);
        return $stmt->execute();
    }
    
    /**
     * Get position for applicant
     */
    public function getPosition($nic, $quarterType = null) {
        if ($quarterType !== null) {
            $sql = "SELECT position FROM {$this->table} WHERE nic = ? AND quarter_type = ? ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1";
            $stmt = $this->conn->prepare($sql);
            $stmt->bind_param("ss", $nic, $quarterType);
        } else {
            $sql = "SELECT position FROM {$this->table} WHERE nic = ? ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1";
            $stmt = $this->conn->prepare($sql);
            $stmt->bind_param("s", $nic);
        }
        $stmt->execute();
        $result = $stmt->get_result();
        $row = $result->fetch_assoc();
        return $row ? $row['position'] : null;
    }

    public function getLatestForNic($nic) {
        $sql = "SELECT position, quarter_type, applied_date, designation_score, employee_marks
                FROM {$this->table} WHERE nic = ? ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1";
        $stmt = $this->conn->prepare($sql);
        $stmt->bind_param("s", $nic);
        $stmt->execute();
        $row = $stmt->get_result()->fetch_assoc();
        $stmt->close();
        return $row;
    }
    
    /**
     * Get waiting list with calculated positions
     */
    public function getWaitingListWithPositions() {
        $sql = "SELECT 
                    a1.nic,
                    a1.quarter_type,
                    a1.applied_date,
                    a1.employee_marks,
                    (SELECT COUNT(*) 
                     FROM waiting_list a2 
                             WHERE a2.quarter_type = a1.quarter_type AND ((a2.applied_date < a1.applied_date)
                                OR (a2.applied_date = a1.applied_date AND a2.employee_marks > a1.employee_marks)
                                OR (a2.applied_date = a1.applied_date AND a2.employee_marks = a1.employee_marks AND a2.id <= a1.id))
                    ) AS position
                FROM waiting_list a1 
                ORDER BY position ASC";
        $result = $this->conn->query($sql);
        return $result->fetch_all(MYSQLI_ASSOC);
    }
    
    public function __destruct() {
        if ($this->conn) {
            $this->conn->close();
        }
    }
}
?>

<!-- JavaScript for Search Logic -->
<script>
    // Sample database simulation for quarters
    const quartersData = {
        "Q-001": {
            no: "Q-001",
            nic: "199012345678",
            compNo: "C-1001",
            type: "Joint Residence",
            name: "S. Vihanga",
            designation: "Station Master",
            workplace: "Colombo Fort",
            allocatedDate: "2024-05-10",
            other: "Primary resident active with shared occupant approval."
        },
        "Q-002": {
            no: "Q-002",
            nic: "198587654321",
            compNo: "C-1002",
            type: "Joint Residence",
            name: "A. B. Perera",
            designation: "Signal Inspector",
            workplace: "Maradana",
            allocatedDate: "2023-11-15",
            other: "Standard joint allocation agreement completed."
        }
    };

    // Function to handle Enter key press event
    function handleKeyPress(event) {
        if (event.key === 'Enter') {
            searchQuarter();
        }
    }

    function searchQuarter() {
        const query = document.getElementById('quarterSearchInput').value.trim();
        const resultBox = document.getElementById('resultContainer');
        const errorMsg = document.getElementById('errorMsg');

        if (quartersData[query]) {
            const data = quartersData[query];
            document.getElementById('resNo').innerText = data.no;
            document.getElementById('resNic').innerText = data.nic;
            document.getElementById('resCompNo').innerText = data.compNo;
            document.getElementById('resType').innerText = data.type;
            document.getElementById('resName').innerText = data.name;
            document.getElementById('resDesignation').innerText = data.designation;
            document.getElementById('resWorkplace').innerText = data.workplace;
            document.getElementById('resDate').innerText = data.allocatedDate;
            document.getElementById('resOther').innerText = data.other;

            resultBox.classList.remove('hidden');
            errorMsg.classList.add('hidden');
        } else {
            resultBox.classList.add('hidden');
            errorMsg.classList.remove('hidden');
        }
    }
</script>

    <script>
        let currentPage = 1;

        window.onload = function() {
            const userData = {
                full_name: "<?php echo htmlspecialchars($_SESSION['full_name'] ?? $_SESSION['user_name'] ?? '', ENT_QUOTES); ?>",
                computer_no: "<?php echo htmlspecialchars($_SESSION['computer_no'] ?? '', ENT_QUOTES); ?>",
                national_id: "<?php echo htmlspecialchars($_SESSION['national_id'] ?? $_SESSION['nic'] ?? '', ENT_QUOTES); ?>",
                phone_no: "<?php echo htmlspecialchars($_SESSION['phone_no'] ?? $_SESSION['user_phone'] ?? '', ENT_QUOTES); ?>"
            };
            
            if (userData.full_name) document.getElementById('full_name').value = userData.full_name;
            if (userData.computer_no) document.getElementById('computer_no').value = userData.computer_no;
            if (userData.national_id) document.getElementById('national_id').value = userData.national_id;
            if (userData.phone_no) document.getElementById('phone_no').value = userData.phone_no;
        };

        function handleEnter(event, nextFieldId) {
            if (event.key === 'Enter') {
                event.preventDefault();
                const nextField = document.getElementsByName(nextFieldId)[0] || document.getElementById(nextFieldId);
                if (nextField) {
                    nextField.focus();
                }
            }
        }

        function setMinDate(fromId, targetIds) {
            const val = document.getElementById(fromId).value;
            targetIds.forEach(id => {
                const el = document.getElementById(id);
                if(el) el.min = val;
            });
        }

        function toggleOwnerRelationship(show) {
            document.getElementById('owner-relationship-group').style.display = show ? 'block' : 'none';
        }

        function showRenameHint(element, message) {
            element.title = message;
        }

        function hideRenameHint(element) {
            // Optional additional logic can go here
        }

        function changePage(currentId, targetId) {
            document.getElementById(currentId).style.display = 'none';
            document.getElementById(targetId).style.display = 'block';
            window.scrollTo(0, 0);
        }

        function toggleTable(tableId, show) {
            document.getElementById(tableId).style.display = show ? "block" : "none";
        }

        function addTableRow(tableId) {
            const table = document.getElementById(tableId).getElementsByTagName('tbody')[0];
            const newRow = table.insertRow();
            const isBreak = tableId === 'table-vi-element';
            const deptName = isBreak ? 'break_sub_department' : 'sub_department_name';
            const fromName = isBreak ? 'break_from_date' : 'service_from_date';
            const toName = isBreak ? 'break_to_date' : 'service_to_date';
            const departments = <?php echo json_encode(array_column($subDepartments, 'name'), JSON_HEX_TAG | JSON_HEX_APOS | JSON_HEX_AMP | JSON_HEX_QUOT); ?>;
            const options = departments.map(name => `<option value="${name}">${name}</option>`).join('');
            newRow.innerHTML = `
                <td><select class="dropdown-select table-select" name="${deptName}[]"><option value="" disabled selected>-- Select --</option>${options}</select></td>
                <td><input type="date" name="${fromName}[]" class="form-input-date from-date" onchange="validateToDate(this)"></td>
                <td><input type="date" name="${toName}[]" class="form-input-date to-date"></td>
            `;
        }

        function toggleContainer(containerId, show) {
            const container = document.getElementById(containerId);
            container.style.display = show ? 'block' : 'none';
            if(containerId === 'children-details' && show) {
                const tbody = document.getElementById('children-tbody');
                if(tbody.rows.length === 0) generateChildRows();
            }
        }

        function handleCivilStatus(status) {
            document.getElementById('married-sections').style.display = 'block';
        }

        function printApplication() {
            window.print();
        }

        // Generate rows based on child count
        function generateChildRows() {
            const countInput = document.getElementById('child-count-input');
            const tbody = document.getElementById('children-tbody');
            tbody.innerHTML = ''; 
            
            const count = parseInt(countInput.value) || 0;
            
            for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
                const row = document.createElement('tr');
                row.innerHTML = `
                    <td><input type="text" class="form-input" name="child_full_name[]" style="width: 100%;"></td>
                    <td>
                        <select class="dropdown-select table-select" name="child_gender[]">
                            <option value="" disabled selected>-- Select --</option>
                            <option value="male">Male</option>
                            <option value="female">Female</option>
                        </select>
                    </td>
                    <td><input type="date" class="form-input-date" name="child_date_of_birth[]"></td>
                    <td>
                        <select class="dropdown-select table-select" name="child_civil_status[]">
                            <option value="" disabled selected>-- Select --</option>
                            <option value="unmarried">Unmarried</option>
                            <option value="married">Married</option>
                            <option value="divorced">Divorced</option>
                            <option value="widowed">Widowed</option>
                        </select>
                    </td>
                    <td>
                        <button type="button" class="clear-row-btn" onclick="clearChildRow(this)">Clear</button>
                    </td>
                `;
                tbody.appendChild(row);
            }
        }

        // Clear data only for the specific row
        function clearChildRow(button) {
            const row = button.closest('tr');
            const inputs = row.querySelectorAll('input, select');
            inputs.forEach(input => {
                if (input.type === 'text' || input.type === 'date') {
                    input.value = '';
                } else if (input.tagName === 'SELECT') {
                    input.selectedIndex = 0;
                }
            });
        }

        // Manage To Date when From Date changes
        function validateToDate(fromInput) {
            const row = fromInput.closest('tr');
            const toInput = row.querySelector('.to-date');
            
            if (fromInput.value) {
                toInput.min = fromInput.value;
                if (toInput.value && toInput.value < fromInput.value) {
                    toInput.value = '';
                }
            } else {
                toInput.removeAttribute('min');
            }
        }
    </script>
</body>
</html>

                    <div class="section-title">10. ප්‍රකාශය </div>
                    <div class="form-group full-width declaration-text" style="font-size: 14px; line-height: 1.8;">
                        <p>මා හට දුම්රිය නිවාසයක් වෙන් කරනු ලැබුවහොත්,</p>
                        <p>I. මගේ පවුලේ සාමාජිකයින් සමග පදිංචිව සිටින බවත්,</p>
                        <p>II. දැනුම් දීමකින් තොරව, නඩත්තු කරුවන් හෝ පිටස්තර පුද්ගලයින් එහි පදිංචි කර නොගන්නා බවත්,</p>
                        <p>III. මෙම ඉල්ලුම්පත්‍රයේ සඳහන් කරුණු (වේතනය වෙනස් වීම, දරුවන්ගේ උපත්, විවාහයන්, අභාවයන්, දික්කසාද වීම හා දරුවන්ගේ කලත්‍රයා රැකියාවක යෙදීම) මින් පසුව වෙනස් වුවහොත්, ඒ පිළිබඳව වාර්තා කිරීමටත්,</p>
                        <p>IV. දෙපාර්තමේන්තුව මගින් දැනුම් දීමෙන් පසුව හෝ අවශ්‍ය වූ විටක, මගේ යැපෙන්නන් සමඟ මෙම නිවාසයෙන් ඉවත්ව, එය හිස් කර භාර දීමේ කොන්දේසියට යටත්ව භාර ගනිමි.</p>
                        
                        <div style="margin-top: 15px; display: inline-block;">
                            ඉහත සඳහන් කර ඇති තොරතුරු සත්‍ය හා නිවැරදි බවත්, වැරදි ප්‍රකාශයක් කළ බවට තහවුරු වුවහොත්, කෙදිනක හෝ මට විරුද්ධව විනයානුකූලව කටයුතු කරනු ලැබීමට ඉඩ ඇති බවට මම දැනුවත් බවත් මෙයින් ප්‍රකාශ කරමි. මම දැනට වෙනත් දුම්රිය නිවාසයක ප්‍රධාන පදිංචිකරු නොවන බව ද සහතික කරමි.

    <script>
        let currentPage = 1;

        function updateHouseNumbers() {
            const location = document.getElementById('location-dropdown').value;
            const houseDropdown = document.getElementById('house-number-dropdown');
            houseDropdown.innerHTML = '<option value="" disabled selected>-- නිවාස අංකය තෝරන්න --</option>';
            
            let houses = [];
            if(location === 'කොළඹ') {
                houses = ['A-101', 'A-102', 'B-205'];
            } else if(location === 'මහනුවර') {
                houses = ['K-01', 'K-02'];
            } else if(location === 'ගාලු') {
                houses = ['G-11', 'G-12'];
            }
            
            houses.forEach(h => {
                let opt = document.createElement('option');
                opt.value = h;
                opt.textContent = h;
                houseDropdown.appendChild(opt);
            });
        }

        function handleEnter(event, nextFieldId) {
            if (event.key === 'Enter') {
                event.preventDefault();
                const nextField = document.getElementsByName(nextFieldId)[0] || document.getElementById(nextFieldId);
                if (nextField) {
                    nextField.focus();
                }
            }
        }

        function setMinDate(fromId, targetIds) {
            const val = document.getElementById(fromId).value;
            targetIds.forEach(id => {
                const el = document.getElementById(id);
                if(el) el.min = val;
            });
        }

        function toggleOwnerRelationship(show) {
            document.getElementById('owner-relationship-group').style.display = show ? 'block' : 'none';
        }

        function showRenameHint(element, message) {
            element.title = message;
        }

        function changePage(currentId, targetId) {
            document.getElementById(currentId).style.display = 'none';
            document.getElementById(targetId).style.display = 'block';
            window.scrollTo(0, 0);
        }

        function toggleTable(tableId, show) {
            document.getElementById(tableId).style.display = show ? "block" : "none";
        }

        function addTableRow(tableId) {
            const table = document.getElementById(tableId).getElementsByTagName('tbody')[0];
            const newRow = table.insertRow();
            const prefix = tableId === 'table-v-element' ? 'table_v' : 'table_vi';
            const departments = <?php echo json_encode(array_column($subDepartments, 'name'), JSON_HEX_TAG | JSON_HEX_APOS | JSON_HEX_AMP | JSON_HEX_QUOT); ?>;
            const options = departments.map(name => `<option value="${name}">${name}</option>`).join('');
            newRow.innerHTML = `
                <td><select class="dropdown-select table-select" name="${prefix}_dept[]"><option value="" disabled selected>-- තෝරන්න --</option>${options}</select></td>
                <td><input type="date" class="form-input-date" name="${prefix}_from[]"></td>
                <td><input type="date" class="form-input-date" name="${prefix}_to[]"></td>
            `;
        }

        function toggleContainer(containerId, show) {
            const container = document.getElementById(containerId);
            container.style.display = show ? 'block' : 'none';
            if(containerId === 'children-details' && show) {
                const tbody = document.getElementById('children-tbody');
                if(tbody.rows.length === 0) addChildRow();
            }
        }

        function handleCivilStatus(status) {
            document.getElementById('married-sections').style.display = 'block';
        }

        function printApplication() {
            const pages = document.querySelectorAll('.page-section');
            pages.forEach(page => {
                page.style.display = 'block';
            });

            window.print();

            pages.forEach((page, index) => {
                if (index !== 0) {
                    page.style.display = 'none';
                }
            });
            document.getElementById(`page-${currentPage}`).style.display = 'block';
        }

        function generateChildRows() {
            const countInput = document.getElementById('child-count-input');
            const tbody = document.getElementById('children-tbody');
            tbody.innerHTML = ''; 
            
            const count = parseInt(countInput.value) || 0;
            
            for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
                const row = document.createElement('tr');
                row.innerHTML = `
                    <td><input type="text" class="form-input" name="child_name[]"></td>
                    <td>
                        <select class="dropdown-select table-select" name="child_gender[]">
                            <option value="" disabled selected>-- තෝරන්න --</option>
                            <option value="male">පුරුෂ</option>
                            <option value="female">ස්ත්‍රී</option>
                        </select>
                    </td>
                    <td><input type="date" class="form-input-date" name="child_dob[]"></td>
                    <td>
                        <select class="dropdown-select table-select" name="child_civil_status[]">
                            <option value="" disabled selected>-- තෝරන්න --</option>
                            <option value="unmarried">අවිවාහක</option>
                            <option value="married">විවාහක</option>
                            <option value="divorced">දික්කසාද</option>
                            <option value="widowed">වැන්දඹු</option>
                        </select>
                    </td>
                    <td>
                        <button type="button" class="clear-row-btn" onclick="clearChildRow(this)">Clear</button>
                    </td>
                `;
                tbody.appendChild(row);
            }
        }

        function clearChildRow(button) {
            const row = button.closest('tr');
            const inputs = row.querySelectorAll('input, select');
            inputs.forEach(input => {
                if (input.type === 'text' || input.type === 'date') {
                    input.value = '';
                } else if (input.tagName === 'SELECT') {
                    input.selectedIndex = 0;
                }
            });
        }

        function validateToDate(fromInput) {
            const row = fromInput.closest('tr');
            const toInput = row.querySelector('.to-date');
            
            if (fromInput.value) {
                toInput.min = fromInput.value;
                if (toInput.value && toInput.value < fromInput.value) {
                    toInput.value = '';
                }
            } else {
                toInput.removeAttribute('min');
            }
        }

    </script>
</body>
</html>

<!-- JavaScript for Tab Switching -->
<script>
    function switchTab(tabName) {
        const contents = document.querySelectorAll('.tab-content');
        contents.forEach(content => content.classList.remove('active'));

        const buttons = document.querySelectorAll('.tab-btn');
        buttons.forEach(btn => btn.classList.remove('active'));

        document.getElementById('content-' + tabName).classList.add('active');
        document.getElementById('btn-' + tabName).classList.add('active');
    }
</script>

<?php
require_once __DIR__ . '/../../controllers/AuthController.php';
$auth = new AuthController();
$auth->requireLogin();

$user = $_SESSION['user_name'] ?? 'User';
$pageTitle = 'Application Successful - Sri Lanka Railways';
include __DIR__ . '/../layouts/header.php';

// Get the application number from URL, fallback to a default
$appNo = isset($_GET['appNo']) ? htmlspecialchars($_GET['appNo'], ENT_QUOTES, 'UTF-8') : null;

// If no app number provided, generate a placeholder
if (empty($appNo)) {
    $currentYear = date('Y');
    $appNo = "SLR/AOT/{$currentYear}/0001";
}
?>

<style>
    /* Subtle fade-in animation for the success card */

        from { opacity: 0; transform: translateY(20px); }
        to   { opacity: 1; transform: translateY(0); }
    }
    .animate-fade-in-up {
        animation: fadeInUp 0.5s ease-out forwards;
    }
    /* Pulse animation for the checkmark */
    @keyframes pulseCheck {
        0%   { transform: scale(0.9); }
        50%  { transform: scale(1.08); }
        100% { transform: scale(1); }
    }
    .animate-check {
        animation: pulseCheck 0.6s ease-in-out 0.3s both;
    }
</style>

File: src/views/auth/login.php

<?php
/**
 * Login Page with Security Features
 */
require_once __DIR__ . '/../../../config/config.php';

// Session is already started in index.php

$pageTitle = 'Login - Quarter Management System';

// If already logged in, redirect to dashboard
if (isset($_SESSION['nic']) && isset($_SESSION['session_valid'])) {
    header('Location: ' . baseUrl('dashboard'));
    exit();
}

$error = '';
$showCaptcha = false;

// Generate CSRF token function
function generateCsrfToken() {
    if (!isset($_SESSION['csrf_token'])) {
        $_SESSION['csrf_token'] = bin2hex(random_bytes(32));
    }
    return $_SESSION['csrf_token'];
}

function verifyCsrfToken($token) {
    if (!isset($_SESSION['csrf_token']) || empty($token)) {
        return false;
    }
    return hash_equals($_SESSION['csrf_token'], $token);
}

// Handle login form submission
if ($_SERVER["REQUEST_METHOD"] == "POST") {
    require_once __DIR__ . '/../../controllers/AuthController.php';
    $auth = new AuthController();
    
    $nic = trim($_POST['nic'] ?? '');
    $password = trim($_POST['password'] ?? '');
    
    // CSRF Protection
    if (!isset($_POST['csrf_token']) || !verifyCsrfToken($_POST['csrf_token'])) {
        $error = 'Security token validation failed. Please try again.';
    } else {
        $result = $auth->login($nic, $password);
        if ($result['success']) {
            // Redirect to intended page or dashboard
            $redirect = $_SESSION['redirect_after_login'] ?? '/dashboard';
            unset($_SESSION['redirect_after_login']);
            
            // Clean the redirect path to avoid duplication
            $redirect = ltrim($redirect, '/');
            // If the redirect is empty or just 'dashboard', use dashboard
            if (empty($redirect) || $redirect === 'dashboard' || $redirect === '/') {
                $redirect = 'dashboard';
            }
            
            header('Location: ' . baseUrl($redirect));
            exit();
        } else {
            $error = $result['message'];
            // Show captcha after 3 failed attempts (client-side tracking)
            $showCaptcha = isset($_POST['attempt_count']) && $_POST['attempt_count'] >= 2;
        }
    }
}

$csrfToken = generateCsrfToken();
?>
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
    <meta charset="UTF-8">
    <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">
    <title><?php echo $pageTitle; ?></title>
    <script src="https://cdn.tailwindcss.com"></script>
    <link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/font-awesome/6.4.0/css/all.min.css">
</head>
<body class="bg-gray-100 min-h-screen flex items-center justify-center">

    <div class="bg-white rounded-2xl shadow-xl p-8 max-w-md w-full border-t-4 border-[#b59410]">
        <!-- Logo -->
        <div class="text-center mb-8">
            <img src="<?php echo assetUrl('images/logo.png'); ?>" alt="Railway Logo" class="h-20 mx-auto mb-4">
            <h1 class="text-2xl font-bold text-[#5c060d]">SRI LANKA RAILWAY</h1>
            <p class="text-sm text-gray-500">Quarter Management System</p>
        </div>

        <?php if ($error): ?>
            <div class="mb-4 p-3 bg-red-50 text-red-700 border border-red-200 rounded-lg text-center">
                <i class="fas fa-exclamation-circle mr-2"></i>
                <?php echo htmlspecialchars($error); ?>
            </div>
        <?php endif; ?>

        <?php if (isset($_GET['registered']) && $_GET['registered'] === 'success'): ?>
            <div class="mb-4 p-3 bg-green-50 text-green-700 border border-green-200 rounded-lg text-center">
                <i class="fas fa-check-circle mr-2"></i>
                Registration successful! Please login.
            </div>
        <?php endif; ?>

        <form method="POST" action="<?php echo baseUrl('login'); ?>" id="loginForm" class="space-y-4">
            <!-- CSRF Token -->
            <input type="hidden" name="csrf_token" value="<?php echo $csrfToken; ?>">
            <input type="hidden" name="attempt_count" id="attemptCount" value="0">
            
            <div>
                <label for="nic" class="block text-sm font-semibold text-gray-700 mb-2">
                    <i class="fas fa-id-card mr-2"></i> NIC Number
                </label>
                <input type="text" 
                       id="nic" 
                       name="nic" 
                       placeholder="Enter your NIC" 
                       value="<?php echo htmlspecialchars($_POST['nic'] ?? ''); ?>"
                       required
                       autocomplete="username"
                       class="w-full px-4 py-3 border border-gray-300 rounded-lg focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#b59410] focus:border-transparent transition">
            </div>

            <div>
                <label for="password" class="block text-sm font-semibold text-gray-700 mb-2">
                    <i class="fas fa-lock mr-2"></i> Password
                </label>
                <div class="relative">
                    <input type="password" 
                           id="password" 
                           name="password" 
                           placeholder="Enter your password"
                           required
                           autocomplete="current-password"
                           class="w-full px-4 py-3 border border-gray-300 rounded-lg focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#b59410] focus:border-transparent transition pr-12">
                    <button type="button" 
                            onclick="togglePasswordVisibility()" 
                            class="absolute right-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-600">
                        <i id="passwordToggleIcon" class="fas fa-eye"></i>
                    </button>
                </div>
            </div>

            <?php if ($showCaptcha): ?>
            <div class="captcha-container bg-gray-50 p-3 rounded-lg border border-gray-200">
                <div class="flex items-center justify-between">
                    <span class="text-sm font-mono" id="captchaText"><?php echo rand(1000, 9999); ?></span>
                    <button type="button" onclick="refreshCaptcha()" class="text-sm text-[#5c060d] hover:underline">
                        <i class="fas fa-sync-alt mr-1"></i> Refresh
                    </button>
                </div>
                <input type="text" 
                       name="captcha" 
                       placeholder="Enter the code above" 
                       class="w-full mt-2 px-4 py-2 border border-gray-300 rounded-lg focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#b59410] focus:border-transparent transition">
                <input type="hidden" name="captcha_hash" id="captchaHash">
            </div>
            <?php endif; ?>

            <button type="submit" 
                    class="w-full bg-[#5c060d] hover:bg-[#4a050a] text-white font-semibold py-3 rounded-lg transition shadow-sm hover:shadow-md hover:text-amber-300"
                    id="loginButton">
                <i class="fas fa-sign-in-alt mr-2"></i> Login
            </button>
        </form>

        <div class="mt-6 pt-4 border-t border-gray-200 text-center space-y-3">
            <p class="text-sm text-gray-600">
                Don't have an account? 
                <a href="<?php echo baseUrl('register'); ?>" class="text-[#5c060d] hover:text-[#b59410] font-semibold">
                    Register here
                </a>
            </p>
            <p class="text-xs text-gray-400">Default test credentials:</p>
            <p class="text-xs text-gray-400 font-mono">NIC: 199012345678 | Password: password123</p>
        </div>
    </div>

    <script>
        let attemptCount = 0;
        
        // Toggle password visibility
        function togglePasswordVisibility() {
            const passwordInput = document.getElementById('password');
            const icon = document.getElementById('passwordToggleIcon');
            
            if (passwordInput.type === 'password') {
                passwordInput.type = 'text';
                icon.className = 'fas fa-eye-slash';
            } else {
                passwordInput.type = 'password';
                icon.className = 'fas fa-eye';
            }
        }
        
        // Track login attempts
        document.getElementById('loginForm').addEventListener('submit', function(e) {
            attemptCount++;
            document.getElementById('attemptCount').value = attemptCount;
        });
        
        // Refresh captcha
        function refreshCaptcha() {
            const captchaText = document.getElementById('captchaText');
            const newCode = Math.floor(1000 + Math.random() * 9000);
            captchaText.textContent = newCode;
        }
        
        // Prevent multiple submissions
        document.getElementById('loginForm').addEventListener('submit', function(e) {
            const button = document.getElementById('loginButton');
            if (button.disabled) {
                e.preventDefault();
                return;
            }
            button.disabled = true;
            button.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-spinner fa-spin mr-2"></i> Logging in...';
        });
    </script>

</body>
</html>

File: src/views/auth/logout.php

<?php
/**
 * Logout Page
 */
require_once __DIR__ . '/../../../config/config.php';

// Session is already started in index.php
// Only start if not already started
if (session_status() === PHP_SESSION_NONE) {
    session_start();
}

session_destroy();

// Redirect to login page
redirect('/login');
exit();
?>

<?php
/**
 * Register Page with Security Features
 */
require_once __DIR__ . '/../../../config/config.php';
require_once __DIR__ . '/../../controllers/AuthController.php';

$pageTitle = 'Register - Quarter Management System';

// If already logged in, redirect to dashboard
if (isset($_SESSION['nic']) && isset($_SESSION['session_valid'])) {
    redirect('/dashboard');
    exit();
}

$error = '';
$success = '';
$formData = [];

// Handle registration form submission
if ($_SERVER["REQUEST_METHOD"] == "POST") {
    $auth = new AuthController();
    
    // CSRF Protection
    if (!isset($_POST['csrf_token']) || !verifyCsrfToken($_POST['csrf_token'])) {
        $error = 'Security token validation failed. Please try again.';
    } else {
        $data = [
            'name' => trim($_POST['name'] ?? ''),
            'nic' => trim($_POST['nic'] ?? ''),
            'email' => trim($_POST['email'] ?? ''),
            'computer_number' => trim($_POST['computer_number'] ?? ''),
            'mobile' => trim($_POST['mobile'] ?? ''),
            'password' => $_POST['password'] ?? '',
            'confirm_password' => $_POST['confirm_password'] ?? ''
        ];
        
        $formData = $data;
        $result = $auth->register($data);
        
        if ($result['success']) {
            // Clear form data
            $formData = [];
            // Redirect to login with success message
            redirect('/login?registered=success');
            exit();
        } else {
            $error = $result['message'];
        }
    }
}

// Generate CSRF token
function generateCsrfToken() {
    if (!isset($_SESSION['csrf_token'])) {
        $_SESSION['csrf_token'] = bin2hex(random_bytes(32));
    }
    return $_SESSION['csrf_token'];
}

function verifyCsrfToken($token) {
    if (!isset($_SESSION['csrf_token']) || empty($token)) {
        return false;
    }
    return hash_equals($_SESSION['csrf_token'], $token);
}

$csrfToken = generateCsrfToken();

                            <i class="fas fa-circle text-[8px]"></i> One lowercase letter
                        </li>
                        <li id="req-number" class="flex items-center gap-1">
                            <i class="fas fa-circle text-[8px]"></i> One number
                        </li>
                    </ul>
                </div>
            </div>

            <!-- Confirm Password -->
            <div>
                <label for="confirm_password" class="block text-sm font-semibold text-gray-700 mb-2">
                    <i class="fas fa-check-circle mr-2"></i> Confirm Password
                </label>
                <div class="relative">
                    <input type="password" 
                           id="confirm_password" 
                           name="confirm_password" 
                           placeholder="Confirm your password" 
                           required
                           class="w-full px-4 py-3 border border-gray-300 rounded-lg focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#b59410] focus:border-transparent transition pr-12">
                    <button type="button" 
                            onclick="togglePasswordVisibility('confirm_password')" 
                            class="absolute right-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-600">
                        <i id="confirmPasswordToggleIcon" class="fas fa-eye"></i>
                    </button>
                </div>
            </div>

            <!-- Terms -->
            <div class="flex items-center">
                <input type="checkbox" 
                       id="terms" 
                       name="terms" 
                       required
                       class="w-4 h-4 text-[#5c060d] border-gray-300 rounded focus:ring-[#5c060d]">
                <label for="terms" class="ml-2 text-sm text-gray-600">
                    I agree to the <a href="#" class="text-[#5c060d] hover:underline">Terms and Conditions</a>
                </label>

                    class="w-full bg-[#5c060d] hover:bg-[#4a050a] text-white font-semibold py-3 rounded-lg transition shadow-sm hover:shadow-md hover:text-amber-300"
                    id="registerButton">
                <i class="fas fa-user-plus mr-2"></i> Register
            </button>
        </form>

        <div class="mt-6 pt-4 border-t border-gray-200 text-center">
            <p class="text-sm text-gray-600">
                Already have an account? 
                <a href="<?php echo baseUrl('login'); ?>" class="text-[#5c060d] hover:text-[#b59410] font-semibold">
                    Login here
                </a>
            </p>
        </div>
    </div>

    <script>
        // Toggle password visibility
        function togglePasswordVisibility(fieldId) {
            const input = document.getElementById(fieldId);
            const icon = document.getElementById(fieldId + 'ToggleIcon');
            
            if (input.type === 'password') {
                input.type = 'text';
                if (icon) icon.className = 'fas fa-eye-slash';
            } else {
                input.type = 'password';
                if (icon) icon.className = 'fas fa-eye';
            }
        }

        // Password strength checker
        const passwordInput = document.getElementById('password');
        const confirmInput = document.getElementById('confirm_password');
        const strengthBar = document.getElementById('strengthBar');
        const strengthText = document.getElementById('strengthText');

        function checkPasswordStrength(password) {
            let score = 0;
            const checks = {
                length: password.length >= 8,
                upper: /[A-Z]/.test(password),
                lower: /[a-z]/.test(password),
                number: /[0-9]/.test(password)
            };
            
            // Update requirements list
            document.getElementById('req-length').innerHTML = 
                `<i class="fas fa-${checks.length ? 'check-circle text-green-500' : 'circle text-[8px]'}"></i> At least 8 characters`;
            document.getElementById('req-upper').innerHTML = 
                `<i class="fas fa-${checks.upper ? 'check-circle text-green-500' : 'circle text-[8px]'}"></i> One uppercase letter`;
            document.getElementById('req-lower').innerHTML = 
                `<i class="fas fa-${checks.lower ? 'check-circle text-green-500' : 'circle text-[8px]'}"></i> One lowercase letter`;
            document.getElementById('req-number').innerHTML = 
                `<i class="fas fa-${checks.number ? 'check-circle text-green-500' : 'circle text-[8px]'}"></i> One number`;
            
            // Calculate score
            if (checks.length) score++;
            if (checks.upper) score++;
            if (checks.lower) score++;
            if (checks.number) score++;
            
            // Update strength bar
            const percentage = (score / 4) * 100;
            strengthBar.style.width = percentage + '%';
            
            const colors = {
                0: 'bg-red-500',
                1: 'bg-red-400',
                2: 'bg-yellow-500',
                3: 'bg-blue-500',
                4: 'bg-green-500'
            };
            const labels = ['', 'Weak', 'Fair', 'Good', 'Strong'];
            
            strengthBar.className = 'h-full rounded-full ' + (colors[score] || 'bg-gray-300');
            strengthText.textContent = labels[score] || '';
            strengthText.className = 'text-xs font-semibold ' + 
                (score >= 3 ? 'text-green-600' : score >= 2 ? 'text-yellow-600' : 'text-red-600');
            
            return checks;
        }

        passwordInput.addEventListener('input', function() {
            checkPasswordStrength(this.value);
        });

        // Confirm password validation
        confirmInput.addEventListener('input', function() {
            if (this.value && this.value !== passwordInput.value) {
                this.className = 'w-full px-4 py-3 border-2 border-red-500 rounded-lg focus:ring-2 focus:ring-red-500 focus:border-transparent transition pr-12';
            } else if (this.value && this.value === passwordInput.value) {
                this.className = 'w-full px-4 py-3 border-2 border-green-500 rounded-lg focus:ring-2 focus:ring-green-500 focus:border-transparent transition pr-12';
            } else {
                this.className = 'w-full px-4 py-3 border border-gray-300 rounded-lg focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#b59410] focus:border-transparent transition pr-12';
            }
        });

        // Prevent multiple submissions
        document.getElementById('registerForm').addEventListener('submit', function(e) {
            const button = document.getElementById('registerButton');
            if (button.disabled) {
                e.preventDefault();
                return;
            }
            
            // Validate password match
            if (passwordInput.value !== confirmInput.value) {
                e.preventDefault();
                alert('Passwords do not match!');
                return;
            }
            
            // Validate terms
            if (!document.getElementById('terms').checked) {
                e.preventDefault();
                alert('Please agree to the Terms and Conditions');
                return;
            }
            
            button.disabled = true;
            button.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-spinner fa-spin mr-2"></i> Registering...';
        });
    </script>

</body>
</html>

            const otherContainer = document.getElementById('other-issue-container');
            const customInput = document.getElementById('custom_issue');
            
            if (this.value === 'other') {
                otherContainer.classList.remove('hidden');
                customInput.setAttribute('required', 'required');
            } else {
                otherContainer.classList.add('hidden');
                customInput.removeAttribute('required');
                customInput.value = ''; // clear input when hidden
            }
