<?php
/**
 * View Status Page
 */
require_once __DIR__ . '/../../controllers/ApplicationController.php';

$appController = new ApplicationController();

// Applications are owned by the user's NIC in the database and session.
$nic = $_SESSION['nic'] ?? null;
$application = $nic ? $appController->getStatus() : null;

$user = [
    'name' => $_SESSION['user_name'] ?? 'User'
];

$userName = $user['name'] ?? 'User';
$pageTitle = 'View Status';

// Fetch waiting list position and marks from application data
$waitingListPosition = $application['position'] ?? 'N/A';
$marks = $application['marks'] ?? '0';

// Define the approval steps with darker/stronger success color themes (thada pata)
$rawSteps = [
    1 => ['name' => 'Application Submit', 'key' => 'submit', 'icon' => 'fa-file-lines', 'contact' => '011 234 5678', 'color' => 'blue', 'bg' => 'bg-blue-600', 'border' => 'border-blue-600', 'badge_bg' => 'bg-blue-200 text-blue-900 border-blue-400', 'row_bg' => 'border-blue-400 bg-blue-100/80'],
    2 => ['name' => 'Immediate Boss', 'key' => 'boss', 'icon' => 'fa-user-tie', 'contact' => '011 234 5679', 'color' => 'purple', 'bg' => 'bg-purple-600', 'border' => 'border-purple-600', 'badge_bg' => 'bg-purple-200 text-purple-900 border-purple-400', 'row_bg' => 'border-purple-400 bg-purple-100/80'],
    3 => ['name' => 'Personal File', 'key' => 'file', 'icon' => 'fa-folder-open', 'contact' => '011 234 5680', 'color' => 'emerald', 'bg' => 'bg-emerald-600', 'border' => 'border-emerald-600', 'badge_bg' => 'bg-emerald-200 text-emerald-900 border-emerald-400', 'row_bg' => 'border-emerald-400 bg-emerald-100/80'],
    4 => ['name' => 'Subject Clerk', 'key' => 'clerk', 'icon' => 'fa-user-pen', 'contact' => '011 234 5681', 'color' => 'indigo', 'bg' => 'bg-indigo-600', 'border' => 'border-indigo-600', 'badge_bg' => 'bg-indigo-200 text-indigo-900 border-indigo-400', 'row_bg' => 'border-indigo-400 bg-indigo-100/80'],
    5 => ['name' => 'Branch Officer', 'key' => 'branch', 'icon' => 'fa-building-columns', 'contact' => '011 234 5682', 'color' => 'amber', 'bg' => 'bg-amber-600', 'border' => 'border-amber-600', 'badge_bg' => 'bg-amber-200 text-amber-900 border-amber-400', 'row_bg' => 'border-amber-400 bg-amber-100/80'],
    6 => ['name' => 'Final Approver', 'key' => 'final', 'icon' => 'fa-award', 'contact' => '011 234 5683', 'color' => 'rose', 'bg' => 'bg-rose-600', 'border' => 'border-rose-600', 'badge_bg' => 'bg-rose-200 text-rose-900 border-rose-400', 'row_bg' => 'border-rose-400 bg-rose-100/80']
];

// Build status array with proper logic
$steps = [];
$isRejectedChain = false;
$inProgressAssigned = false;

// Get statuses from application or default to pending
$subStatus = $application['submit_status'] ?? (
    ($application['application_status'] ?? '') === 'submitted' ? 'success' : 'pending'
);
$bStatus = $application['boss_status'] ?? 'pending';
$fStatus = $application['file_status'] ?? 'pending';
$cStatus = $application['clerk_status'] ?? 'pending';
$brStatus = $application['branch_status'] ?? 'pending';
$fnStatus = $application['final_status'] ?? 'pending';

$statusMap = [
    1 => $subStatus,
    2 => $bStatus,
    3 => $fStatus,
    4 => $cStatus,
    5 => $brStatus,
    6 => $fnStatus
];

foreach ($rawSteps as $id => $stepInfo) {
    $st = $statusMap[$id] ?? 'pending';
    
    // If any step is rejected, all subsequent steps become rejected
    if ($isRejectedChain) {
        $st = 'rejected';
    }
    
    if ($st === 'rejected' && !$isRejectedChain) {
        $isRejectedChain = true;
    }

    // Mark the first pending step as 'in-progress'
    if (!$isRejectedChain) {
        if ($st === 'pending' && !$inProgressAssigned) {
            $st = 'in-progress';
            $inProgressAssigned = true;
        } elseif ($st === 'in-progress') {
            $inProgressAssigned = true;
        }
    }

    // Get rejection reason if applicable
    $stepReason = null;
    if ($st === 'rejected') {
        $stepReason = $application[$stepInfo['key'] . '_reason'] ?? 'This step was rejected or stopped due to previous stage issues.';
    }

    $steps[$id] = [
        'name' => $stepInfo['name'],
        'icon' => $stepInfo['icon'],
        'status' => $st,
        'contact' => $stepInfo['contact'],
        'reason' => $stepReason,
        'color' => $stepInfo['color'],
        'bg' => $stepInfo['bg'],
        'border' => $stepInfo['border'],
        'badge_bg' => $stepInfo['badge_bg'],
        'row_bg' => $stepInfo['row_bg']
    ];
}

// Conditions for showing Marks and Waiting List Position
$isSubmitted = ($subStatus === 'success' || $subStatus === 'approved');
$isFinalApproved = ($fnStatus === 'success' || $fnStatus === 'approved');

// Set flag to hide dashboard-specific header action icons/profile on this inner page
$hideHeaderActions = true;

include __DIR__ . '/../layouts/header.php';
?>

    <!-- Main Container -->
    <main class="max-w-6xl w-full mx-auto px-4 py-8 flex-grow">
        <div class="bg-white rounded-xl shadow-lg border border-gray-200 p-8">
            
            <!-- Title Bar -->
            <div class="flex justify-between items-center mb-6 border-b pb-4">
                <div class="flex items-center space-x-3">
                    <div class="text-[#5a0b13] text-2xl">
                        <i class="fa-solid fa-file-lines"></i>
                    </div>
                    <div>
                        <h2 class="text-2xl font-bold text-gray-800">View Status</h2>
                        <p class="text-sm text-gray-500">Track your application verification progress.</p>
                    </div>
                </div>
                <a href="<?php echo baseUrl('dashboard'); ?>" class="bg-[#5a0b13] text-white text-sm px-4 py-2 rounded-lg hover:bg-opacity-95 transition flex items-center space-x-2 shadow">
                    <i class="fa-solid fa-arrow-left"></i>
                    <span>Back to Dashboard</span>
                </a>
            </div>

            <?php if ($application): ?>
                <!-- Progress Tracker Bar -->
                <div class="mb-12 relative px-6 md:px-12">
                    <?php
                        $totalSteps = count($steps);
                        $progressPercent = 0;
                        if ($totalSteps > 1) {
                            $successSteps = 0;
                            foreach ($steps as $stItem) {
                                if ($stItem['status'] == 'success' || $stItem['status'] == 'approved') {
                                    $successSteps++;
                                }
                            }
                            $progressPercent = ($successSteps / ($totalSteps - 1)) * 100;
                            if ($progressPercent > 100) $progressPercent = 100;
                        }
                    ?>
                    <!-- Background Gray Line -->
                    <div class="absolute top-1/2 left-12 right-12 h-1.5 bg-gray-200 -translate-y-1/2 z-0 rounded-full"></div>
                    
                    <!-- Dynamic Colored Active Progress Line (Darker/Stronger emerald-600) -->
                    <div class="absolute top-1/2 left-12 h-1.5 bg-emerald-600 -translate-y-1/2 z-0 rounded-full transition-all duration-500" style="width: calc(<?= $progressPercent; ?>% - 24px);"></div>

                    <div class="relative z-10 flex justify-between items-center">
                        <?php foreach ($steps as $id => $step): ?>
                        <div class="flex flex-col items-center">
                            <!-- Top Icon Circle -->
                            <div class="w-12 h-12 rounded-full border-2 flex items-center justify-center text-base <?= $step['bg']; ?> text-white <?= $step['border']; ?> shadow-md relative transition-all duration-300
                                <?php if ($step['status'] == 'in-progress'): ?> ring-4 ring-<?= $step['color']; ?>-100 shadow-<?= $step['color']; ?>-100
                                <?php elseif ($step['status'] == 'rejected'): ?> ring-4 ring-rose-100 shadow-rose-100
                                <?php endif; ?>">
                                <?php if ($step['status'] == 'success' || $step['status'] == 'approved'): ?>
                                    <i class="fa-solid fa-check text-sm"></i>
                                <?php elseif ($step['status'] == 'in-progress'): ?>
                                    <i class="fa-solid fa-clock text-sm"></i>
                                <?php elseif ($step['status'] == 'rejected'): ?>
                                    <i class="fa-solid fa-xmark text-sm"></i>
                                <?php else: ?>
                                    <i class="fa-solid <?= $step['icon']; ?> text-xs"></i>
                                <?php endif; ?>
                            </div>
                            <span class="text-xs font-semibold mt-2 text-gray-700 text-center max-w-[90px]"><?= $step['name']; ?></span>
                            
                            <!-- Bottom Number Badge -->
                            <div class="mt-1 w-5 h-5 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center text-[10px] font-bold text-white <?= $step['bg']; ?>">
                                <?= $id; ?>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                        <?php endforeach; ?>
                    </div>
                </div>

                <!-- Status Rows List (Only shows active, in-progress, completed, or rejected steps) -->
                <div class="space-y-4">
                    <?php foreach ($steps as $id => $step): 
                        // Hide pending future steps from the list below
                        if ($step['status'] == 'pending') {
                            continue;
                        }

                        $borderCol = "border-gray-200 bg-white";
                        $badgeBg = "bg-gray-100 text-gray-600 border-gray-300";
                        $badgeText = "Pending";

                        if ($step['status'] == 'success' || $step['status'] == 'approved') {
                            $borderCol = $step['row_bg'];
                            $badgeBg = $step['badge_bg'];
                            $badgeText = "Success";
                        } elseif ($step['status'] == 'in-progress') {
                            $borderCol = "border-2 " . $step['border'] . " bg-white";
                            $badgeBg = $step['badge_bg'];
                            $badgeText = "In Progress";
                        } elseif ($step['status'] == 'rejected') {
                            $borderCol = "border-rose-200 bg-rose-50/30";
                            $badgeBg = "bg-rose-100 text-rose-800 border-rose-300";
                            $badgeText = "Rejected";
                        }
                    ?>
                    <div class="border <?= $borderCol; ?> rounded-xl p-4 flex flex-col md:flex-row items-center justify-between gap-4 shadow-sm">
                        <!-- Icon & Description -->
                        <div class="flex items-center space-x-4 w-full md:w-2/4">
                            <div class="w-12 h-12 rounded-full bg-white border border-gray-200 flex items-center justify-center text-xl shadow-sm text-gray-700 flex-shrink-0">
                                <i class="fa-solid <?= $step['icon']; ?>"></i>
                            </div>
                            <div>
                                <div class="flex items-center space-x-2">
                                    <span class="font-bold text-gray-800"><?= $id; ?>. <?= $step['name']; ?></span>
                                </div>
                                <p class="text-xs text-gray-500 mt-0.5">
                                    <?php if ($step['status'] == 'success' || $step['status'] == 'approved'): ?>
                                        Your application has been successfully verified by <?= $step['name']; ?>.
                                    <?php elseif ($step['status'] == 'in-progress'): ?>
                                        Your application is currently being processed at <?= $step['name']; ?>.
                                    <?php elseif ($step['status'] == 'rejected'): ?>
                                        <?php if (!empty($step['reason'])): ?>
                                            <span class="text-rose-600 font-semibold">Reject Reason:</span> <?= htmlspecialchars($step['reason']); ?>
                                        <?php else: ?>
                                            This step was marked as rejected or stopped due to previous stage issues.
                                        <?php endif; ?>
                                    <?php else: ?>
                                        The <?= $step['name']; ?> verification is required and is pending in the current process.

                            </div>
                        </div>

                        <!-- Status Badge -->
                        <div class="w-full md:w-1/4 text-center">

                            </span>
                        </div>

                        </div>

                </div>

                                    <p class="text-xs text-gray-500">Current waiting list position</p>

                        </div>

                            <div class="flex items-center justify-between">

 * Admin Footer Layout

    <footer class="bg-[#5c060d] text-white/85 text-center py-5 mt-auto border-t-2 border-[#b59410]/40 shadow-inner w-full">
        <p class="text-sm font-medium">&copy; <?php echo date('Y'); ?> Department of Railways - Quarter Management System (Admin Panel). All rights reserved.</p>
    </footer>

    <!-- Custom JavaScript for Admin Dropdown -->
    <script>
        function toggleAdminDropdown() {
            const dropdown = document.getElementById('adminProfileDropdown');
            if (dropdown) {
                dropdown.classList.toggle('hidden');
            }
        }

        // Close dropdown when clicking outside
        window.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
            const dropdown = document.getElementById('adminProfileDropdown');
            const container = document.querySelector('.profile-dropdown-container');
            if (dropdown && container && !container.contains(e.target)) {
                dropdown.classList.add('hidden');
            }
        });
    </script>
    <script src="<?php echo assetUrl('js/main.js'); ?>"></script>
</body>
</html>

<?php
/**
 * Admin Header Layout
 */
require_once __DIR__ . '/../../../config/config.php';

if (session_status() === PHP_SESSION_NONE) {
    session_start();
}

if (!isset($_SESSION['admin_name'])) {
    $_SESSION['admin_name'] = 'Admin Name';
}

$admin_name = $_SESSION['admin_name'];

// Initialize $user array if not already set, using session admin_name[cite: 2]
if (!isset($user) || !isset($user['name'])) {
    $user = ['name' => $admin_name];
}

$pageTitle = $pageTitle ?? 'Admin Dashboard - Sri Lanka Railway';

// Agar page ne manually $hide_admin_extras set nahi kiya hai, tabhi automatic check chale[cite: 2]
if (!isset($hide_admin_extras)) {
    $hide_admin_extras = (basename($_SERVER['PHP_SELF']) !== 'dashboard.php');
}
?>
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">

    <!-- Top Dark Red Header Bar for Admin -->

        <div class="flex items-center space-x-4 mb-4 md:mb-0">
            <img src="<?php echo assetUrl('images/logo.png'); ?>" alt="Sri Lanka Railway Logo" class="slr-logo">

                <h1 class="text-xl md:text-2xl font-bold tracking-wider">SRI LANKA RAILWAY</h1>
                <h2 class="text-sm md:text-base font-semibold tracking-wide text-amber-200">QUARTER MANAGEMENT SYSTEM</h2>
            </div>
        </div>

        <?php if (!$hide_admin_extras): ?>
        <!-- Centered Admin Dashboard Title -->

            <span class="text-xl font-extrabold tracking-wide uppercase m-0">ADMIN DASHBOARD</span>

<?php
/**
 * Notifications Page
 */
require_once __DIR__ . '/../../controllers/NotificationController.php';

$notificationController = new NotificationController();
$notifications = $notificationController->getAll();

// Handle AJAX requests
if ($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] === 'POST' && isset($_POST['ajax_action'])) {
    header('Content-Type: application/json');
    $targetId = isset($_POST['action_id']) ? (int)$_POST['action_id'] : 0;
    
    if ($_POST['ajax_action'] === 'mark_read' && $targetId > 0) {
        $result = $notificationController->markAsRead($targetId);
        echo json_encode($result);
        exit();
    } 
    elseif ($_POST['ajax_action'] === 'delete' && $targetId > 0) {
        $result = $notificationController->delete($targetId);
        echo json_encode($result);
        exit();
    }
}

$user = $_SESSION['user_name'] ?? 'User';
$pageTitle = 'Notifications';

include __DIR__ . '/../layouts/header.php';
?>

    <div class="max-w-4xl mx-auto px-4 py-12">
        <div class="bg-white rounded-2xl shadow-sm border border-gray-200 p-8 hover:border-amber-400 transition-all duration-300">
            <!-- Back to Dashboard Button (Top-Left) -->
            <div class="mb-6">
                <a href="<?php echo baseUrl('dashboard'); ?>" class="inline-flex items-center bg-[#5c060d] hover:bg-[#4a050a] text-white hover:text-amber-300 font-semibold px-4 py-2 rounded-lg transition shadow-sm hover:shadow-md text-sm">
                    <i class="fa-solid fa-arrow-left mr-2"></i> Back to Dashboard
                </a>
            </div>

            <h2 class="text-2xl font-bold text-gray-900 text-center mb-2">🔔 Notifications</h2>
            <p class="text-gray-500 text-center text-sm mb-6">Stay updated with your application status</p>

            <div id="notificationsContainer" class="space-y-4">
                <?php if (!empty($notifications)): ?>
                    <?php foreach ($notifications as $notif): ?>
                        <div id="notif-box-<?php echo $notif['id']; ?>" 
                             class="bg-white border border-gray-200 border-l-4 border-l-amber-400 rounded-lg p-5 hover:shadow-md transition">
                            <div class="flex justify-between items-start mb-2">
                                <span class="text-xs text-gray-400">
                                    <i class="far fa-calendar-alt mr-1"></i> 
                                    <?php echo htmlspecialchars($notif['created_at'] ?? date('Y-m-d H:i')); ?>
                                </span>
                                <span id="status-text-<?php echo $notif['id']; ?>">
                                    <?php if ($notif['is_read'] == 1): ?>
                                        <span class="text-xs font-semibold text-green-600 bg-green-50 px-3 py-1 rounded-full border border-green-200">
                                            <i class="fas fa-check-circle mr-1"></i> Read
                                        </span>
                                    <?php else: ?>
                                        <span class="text-xs font-semibold text-red-600 bg-red-50 px-3 py-1 rounded-full border border-red-200">
                                            <i class="fas fa-exclamation-circle mr-1"></i> Unread
                                        </span>
                                    <?php endif; ?>
                                </span>
                            </div>
                            <h4 class="font-bold text-gray-800 text-lg mb-2">
                                <?php echo htmlspecialchars($notif['title'] ?? 'Notification'); ?>
                            </h4>
                            <p class="text-gray-600 text-sm mb-4">
                                <?php echo htmlspecialchars($notif['message'] ?? ''); ?>
                            </p>
                            <div class="flex items-center justify-end space-x-3">
                                <div id="mark-read-btn-wrap-<?php echo $notif['id']; ?>">
                                    <?php if ($notif['is_read'] == 0): ?>
                                        <button type="button" onclick="markAsRead(<?php echo $notif['id']; ?>)" 
                                                class="px-4 py-2 bg-amber-50 hover:bg-amber-100 text-amber-700 font-semibold text-sm rounded-lg transition border border-amber-200">
                                            <i class="fas fa-check mr-1"></i> Mark as read
                                        </button>
                                    <?php endif; ?>
                                </div>
                                <button type="button" data-id="<?php echo $notif['id']; ?>" 
                                        class="delete-trigger-btn px-4 py-2 bg-red-50 hover:bg-red-100 text-red-600 font-semibold text-sm rounded-lg transition border border-red-200">
                                    <i class="fas fa-trash-alt mr-1"></i> Delete
                                </button>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    <?php endforeach; ?>
                <?php else: ?>
                    <div class="text-center py-12 text-gray-400 bg-gray-50 rounded-xl border border-dashed border-gray-300">
                        <p class="text-lg">No notifications available at the moment.</p>
                    </div>
                <?php endif; ?>
            </div>
        </div>
    </div>

    <!-- Modern Notification Delete Confirmation Modal -->
    <div id="deleteModal" style="display: none;" class="fixed inset-0 bg-black/60 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-center justify-center z-50 transition-all duration-300">
        <div class="bg-white rounded-3xl shadow-2xl max-w-md w-full p-8 text-center transform transition-all border border-gray-100 mx-4">
            <div class="flex justify-center mb-5">
                <div class="w-16 h-16 rounded-2xl bg-red-50 flex items-center justify-center text-red-600 text-2xl shadow-inner border border-red-100">
                    <i class="fas fa-trash-alt"></i>
                </div>
            </div>
            <h3 class="text-xl font-bold text-gray-900 mb-2">Delete Notification</h3>
            <p class="text-gray-500 text-sm mb-8 leading-relaxed">Are you sure you want to delete this message? </p>
            <div class="flex items-center justify-center gap-3">
                <button id="cancelDeleteBtn" type="button" class="flex-1 px-5 py-3 bg-gray-100 hover:bg-gray-200 text-gray-700 font-semibold rounded-xl transition-all duration-200 text-sm">
                    Cancel
                </button>
                <button id="confirmDeleteBtn" type="button" class="flex-1 px-5 py-3 bg-red-600 hover:bg-red-700 text-white font-semibold rounded-xl transition-all duration-200 shadow-lg shadow-red-600/25 text-sm">
                    Yes, Delete
                </button>
            </div>
        </div>
    </div>

    <script>
        let currentDeleteId = null;
        const deleteModal = document.getElementById('deleteModal');

        // Event listener for all delete buttons
        document.addEventListener('click', function(event) {
            let btn = event.target.closest('.delete-trigger-btn');
            if (btn) {
                currentDeleteId = btn.getAttribute('data-id');
                deleteModal.style.display = 'flex';
            }
        });

        // Modal Cancel Button Event
        document.getElementById('cancelDeleteBtn').addEventListener('click', () => {
            deleteModal.style.display = 'none';
            currentDeleteId = null;
        });

        // Modal Confirm Delete Button Event
        document.getElementById('confirmDeleteBtn').addEventListener('click', () => {
            deleteModal.style.display = 'none';
            if (currentDeleteId) {
                executeAction(currentDeleteId, 'delete');
                currentDeleteId = null;
            }
        });

        function markAsRead(id) {
            executeAction(id, 'mark_read');
        }

        function executeAction(id, action) {
            let formData = new URLSearchParams();
            formData.append('ajax_action', action);
            formData.append('action_id', id);

            fetch('', {
                method: 'POST',
                body: formData
            })
            .then(response => response.json())
            .then(data => {
                if (data.status === 'success') {
                    if (action === 'mark_read') {
                        document.getElementById('status-text-' + id).innerHTML = 
                            '<span class="text-xs font-semibold text-green-600 bg-green-50 px-3 py-1 rounded-full border border-green-200"><i class="fas fa-check-circle mr-1"></i> Read</span>';
                        let readBtnWrap = document.getElementById('mark-read-btn-wrap-' + id);
                        if (readBtnWrap) readBtnWrap.innerHTML = '';
                    } else if (action === 'delete') {
                        let box = document.getElementById('notif-box-' + id);
                        if (box) box.remove();
                        
                        let container = document.getElementById('notificationsContainer');
                        if (container && container.querySelectorAll('[id^="notif-box-"]').length === 0) {
                            location.reload();
                        }
                    }
                }
            })
            .catch(error => {
                console.error('Error:', error);
            });
        }
    </script>

<?php include __DIR__ . '/../layouts/footer.php'; ?>

File: src/views/profile/edit_profile.php

<?php
/**
 * Edit Profile Page
 */
require_once __DIR__ . '/../../controllers/ProfileController.php';

$profileController = new ProfileController();
$userData = $profileController->getProfile();
$error = '';
$success = '';

if ($_SERVER["REQUEST_METHOD"] == "POST") {
    $email = trim($_POST['email'] ?? '');
    $computer_no = trim($_POST['computer_no'] ?? '');
    $mobile = trim($_POST['mobile'] ?? '');
    $whatsapp = trim($_POST['whatsapp'] ?? '');
    $password = $_POST['password'] ?? '';
    $confirm_password = $_POST['confirm_password'] ?? '';
    
    // Retain existing Name and NIC since users are not allowed to change them
    $name = $userData['name'] ?? '';
    $nic = $userData['nic'] ?? '';
    
    if (!empty($password) && $password !== $confirm_password) {
        $error = "Passwords do not match! Please check again.";
    } elseif ($email && $computer_no && $mobile && $whatsapp) {
        $result = $profileController->updateProfile($name, $nic, $email, $computer_no, $mobile, $whatsapp, $password);
        if ($result['success']) {
            // Update database and automatically redirect to dashboard on success
            header("Location: " . baseUrl('dashboard'));
            exit();
        } else {
            $error = $result['message'];
        }
    } else {
        $error = 'Please fill in all required fields.';
    }
}

$pageTitle = 'Edit Profile';

// Include layout header
include __DIR__ . '/../layouts/header.php';
?>

    <div class="max-w-5xl mx-auto px-4 py-10">
        <!-- Wide Card Box with Two-Column Grid Fields -->
        <div class="bg-white p-8 sm:p-12 rounded-2xl shadow-xl border-t-8 border-[#7A1C1C]">
            <div class="text-center mb-8">
                <span class="inline-block p-3 bg-red-50 text-[#7A1C1C] rounded-full mb-2 text-xl shadow-sm">
                    <i class="fa-solid fa-pen-to-square"></i>
                </span>
                <h2 class="text-2xl font-bold text-gray-800 uppercase">Edit Profile</h2>

                </div>
            <?php endif; ?>
            
            <?php if (!empty($success)): ?>
                <div class="mb-6 p-4 rounded-lg text-sm font-medium bg-green-100 text-green-700">
                    <?php echo htmlspecialchars($success); ?>
                </div>
            <?php endif; ?>

            <form method="POST" action="" onsubmit="return validateForm(event)">
                <!-- Two Column Grid Layout -->
                <div class="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-6">
                    <!-- Computer Number (with PC/Desktop icon) -->
                    <div>
                        <label class="block text-sm font-semibold text-gray-700 mb-1">Computer Number</label>
                        <div class="relative">
                            <span class="absolute inset-y-0 left-0 pl-3.5 flex items-center pointer-events-none text-gray-400">
                                <i class="fa-solid fa-desktop"></i>
                            </span>
                            <input type="text" name="computer_no" value="<?php echo htmlspecialchars($userData['computer_number'] ?? ''); ?>" required

    <script>
        function validateForm(event) {
            const password = document.getElementById('password').value;
            const confirmPassword = document.getElementById('confirmPassword').value;

            if (password !== "" && password !== confirmPassword) {
                alert("Passwords do not match! Please check again.");
                document.getElementById('confirmPassword').focus();
                event.preventDefault();
                return false;
            }
            return true;
        }
    </script>
